Implement the following plan:

# Plan: `entire trail` command

## Context

PR #738 in entire.io introduces a "Trails" system on the web — a branch-centric work tracking abstraction stored on an `entire/trails` orphan branch. Trails answer "why/what" (human intent) while Checkpoints answer "how/when" (machine snapshots). They're linked by branch name.

This plan adds the CLI counterpart: an `entire trail` command that creates and manages trail metadata locally in git. When pushed (via pre-push hook), the web UI picks it up via webhooks automatically. Additionally, trails are auto-created when an agent session starts on a non-main branch.

## Command Structure

```
entire trail              # Show current branch's trail (or list if none)
entire trail list          # List all trails (with optional --status filter)
entire trail create        # Create a trail for current or new branch
entire trail update        # Update trail metadata (status, title, etc.)
```

## Key Design Decisions

- **Storage**: Dedicated `entire/trails` orphan branch (matches web PR)
- **ID format**: 12 hex char IDs with `<id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/` sharding (same as checkpoints)
- **Auto-create**: On session start (UserPromptSubmit hook), if on a non-main branch without a trail, auto-create one with status `in_progress`
- **Push**: Pre-push hook pushes `entire/trails` alongside `entire/checkpoints/v1`
- **Linking**: Trail ↔ Checkpoints linked by branch name (no foreign key)

## Data Format

### `entire/trails` branch structure
```
<trail_id[:2]>/<trail_id[2:]>/
  metadata.json
  discussion.json    # Empty initially, comments come later
```

### `metadata.json` (matches web PR format)
```json
{
  "trail_id": "abcdef123456",
  "branch": "feature/auth",
  "base": "main",
  "title": "Add user authentication",
  "description": "",
  "status": "in_progress",
  "author": "alice",
  "assignees": [],
  "labels": [],
  "created_at": "2026-02-18T10:30:00Z",
  "updated_at": "2026-02-20T14:22:00Z"
}
```

### Trail Status Lifecycle
```
draft → open → in_progress → in_review → done → closed
```

## Implementation Steps

### 1. Add `TrailsBranchName` constant
**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/paths/paths.go` (line 38)

Add `TrailsBranchName = "entire/trails"` alongside existing `MetadataBranchName`.

### 2. Create trail package
**New directory**: `cmd/entire/cli/trail/`

#### `trail.go` — Types and helpers
- `TrailID` type (12 hex chars, same pattern as `checkpoint/id.CheckpointID` in `cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/id/id.go`)
  - `Generate()`, `Validate()`, `Path()`, `String()`, `IsEmpty()`
- `TrailStatus` type with constants: `StatusDraft`, `StatusOpen`, `StatusInProgress`, `StatusInReview`, `StatusDone`, `StatusClosed`
  - `ValidStatuses()` and `IsValid()` for validation
- `TrailMetadata` struct matching web PR format (trail_id, branch, base, title, description, status, author, assignees, labels, created_at, updated_at, merged_at)
- `TrailDiscussion` struct (initially `Comments []TrailComment` — empty, ready for future use)
- `TrailComment` and `TrailCommentReply` structs (matching web PR)
- `HumanizeBranchName(branch string) string` helper
  - Strip common prefixes (`feature/`, `fix/`, `bugfix/`, `chore/`, `hotfix/`, `release/`)
  - Replace `-`/`_` with spaces, capitalize first word

#### `store.go` — Git storage operations
`TrailStore` wrapping `*git.Repository`:
- `NewTrailStore(repo)` constructor
- `EnsureBranch()` — create `entire/trails` orphan branch if needed
  - Follow pattern from `checkpoint.GitStore.ensureSessionsBranch()` (`cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/committed.go:1264`)
- `Write(ctx, metadata, discussion)` — write metadata.json + discussion.json to sharded path
- `Read(ctx, trailID)` — read a single trail by ID
- `FindByBranch(ctx, branchName)` — find trail for a branch (scan all metadata.json files)
- `List(ctx)` — list all trails
- `Update(ctx, metadata)` — update existing trail (read-modify-write)
- `Delete(ctx, trailID)` — remove trail from tree

Reuse exported functions from checkpoint package:
- `checkpoint.CreateBlobFromContent()` — `cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/committed.go:1320`
- `checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries()` — `cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/temporary.go` (exported)
- `checkpoint.FlattenTree()` — `cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/temporary.go` (exported)
- `checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo()` — `cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/committed.go:1457`

### 3. Create trail command
**New file**: `cmd/entire/cli/trail_cmd.go`

#### `entire trail` (root, no subcommand)
- If on a branch with a trail → show trail details (title, status, branch, base, timestamps)
- If no trail for current branch → fall through to `entire trail list` output

#### `entire trail list`
- Scan `entire/trails` branch, list all trails
- Flags: `--status <status>` (filter), `--json` (JSON output)
- Table output: branch, title, status, author, created_at

#### `entire trail create`
- Interactive mode (default): prompt for title, description, base branch
  - Title defaults to `HumanizeBranchName(currentBranch)`
  - Base defaults to detected default branch — reuse `strategy.GetDefaultBranchName()` (`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go:1447`)
  - Uses `NewAccessibleForm()` for accessibility (`cmd/entire/cli/utils.go`)
- Non-interactive flags: `--title`, `--description`, `--base`, `--branch`, `--status`
  - If `--branch` specified and branch doesn't exist, create it via `git checkout -b`
  - If no `--branch`, use current branch via `GetCurrentBranch()` (`cmd/entire/cli/git_operations.go:156`)
  - Error if on main/master — use `IsOnDefaultBranch()` (`cmd/entire/cli/git_operations.go:83`)
- Initial status: `StatusDraft` for explicit create, `StatusInProgress` for auto-create

#### `entire trail update`
- Requires being on a branch with a trail (or `--branch` flag)
- Flags: `--status`, `--title`, `--description`, `--label add:X`, `--label remove:X`
- Updates `updated_at` timestamp automatically

### 4. Auto-create trail on session start
**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go` — in `handleLifecycleTurnStart()` (line 112)

Insert after session initialization (line ~145), before return:
1. Get current branch via `GetCurrentBranch()` (`cmd/entire/cli/git_operations.go:156`)
2. Check if on default branch via `IsOnDefaultBranch()` — skip if true or error
3. Open trail store, call `FindByBranch(ctx, branchName)`
4. If trail already exists, skip
5. If no trail, auto-create:
   - Trail ID: `trail.GenerateID()`
   - Branch: current branch name
   - Base: `strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo)` — need to open repo first
   - Title: `trail.HumanizeBranchName(branchName)`
   - Status: `trail.StatusInProgress`
   - Author: git user.name from `checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo()`
6. Write trail via store
7. Log: `fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created trail for branch: %s\n", branchName)`
8. Non-blocking: wrap in helper, log errors but don't fail the session

### 5. Push trails in pre-push hook
**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/push_common.go`

Add `PushTrailsBranch(remote string) error` function:
- Reuse `pushSessionsBranchCommon()` pattern (line 26) but with `paths.TrailsBranchName`
- Same logic: check if branch exists locally, compare with remote, push with conflict resolution

Update pre-push handlers to also call `PushTrailsBranch()`:
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_push.go:12` — add after existing push
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go:120` — add after existing push

### 6. Register trail command
**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/root.go` (line 86, before help command)

Add `cmd.AddCommand(newTrailCmd())`.

### 7. Update CLAUDE.md
Document the new `trail` package, command structure, and `entire/trails` branch.

## Verification

1. **Unit tests**: `mise run test` — trail package types, store operations, humanize
2. **Integration tests**: Create trail, list, update, verify on `entire/trails` branch
3. **Manual test flow**:
   ```bash
   git checkout -b feature/test-trail
   entire trail create --title "Test trail"
   entire trail list
   entire trail update --status in_review
   entire trail
   ```
4. **Auto-create**: Start a session on a branch, verify trail is created
5. **Push**: `git push` and verify `entire/trails` is pushed
6. **Lint**: `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci`

    37→// MetadataBranchName is the orphan branch used by auto-commit and manual-commit strategies to store metadata

    40→// CheckpointPath returns the sharded storage path for a checkpoint ID.
    41→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
    42→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
    43→//
    44→// Deprecated: Use checkpointID.Path() directly instead.
    45→func CheckpointPath(checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {
    46→	return checkpointID.Path()
    47→}
    48→
    49→// repoRootCache caches the repository root to avoid repeated git commands.
    50→// The cache is keyed by the current working directory to handle directory changes.
    51→var (
    52→	repoRootMu       sync.RWMutex
    53→	repoRootCache    string
    54→	repoRootCacheDir string
    55→)
    56→
    57→// RepoRoot returns the git repository root directory.
    58→// Uses 'git rev-parse --show-toplevel' which works from any subdirectory.
    59→// The result is cached per working directory.
    60→// Returns an error if not inside a git repository.
    61→func RepoRoot() (string, error) {
    62→	// Get current working directory to check cache validity
    63→	cwd, err := os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // already present in codebase
    64→	if err != nil {
    65→		cwd = ""
    66→	}
    67→
    68→	// Check cache with read lock first
    69→	repoRootMu.RLock()
    70→	if repoRootCache != "" && repoRootCacheDir == cwd {
    71→		cached := repoRootCache
    72→		repoRootMu.RUnlock()
    73→		return cached, nil
    74→	}
    75→	repoRootMu.RUnlock()
    76→
    77→	// Cache miss - get repo root and update cache with write lock
    78→	ctx := context.Background()
    79→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
    80→	output, err := cmd.Output()
    81→	if err != nil {
    82→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git repository root: %w", err)
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	root := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
    86→
    87→	repoRootMu.Lock()
    88→	repoRootCache = root
    89→	repoRootCacheDir = cwd
    90→	repoRootMu.Unlock()
    91→
    92→	return root, nil
    93→}
    94→
    95→// ClearRepoRootCache clears the cached repository root.
    96→// This is primarily useful for testing when changing directories.
    97→func ClearRepoRootCache() {
    98→	repoRootMu.Lock()
    99→	repoRootCache = ""
   100→	repoRootCacheDir = ""
   101→	repoRootMu.Unlock()
   102→}
   103→
   104→// AbsPath returns the absolute path for a relative path within the repository.
   105→// If the path is already absolute, it is returned as-is.
   106→// Uses RepoRoot() to resolve paths relative to the repository root.
   107→func AbsPath(relPath string) (string, error) {
   108→	if filepath.IsAbs(relPath) {
   109→		return relPath, nil
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	root, err := RepoRoot()
   113→	if err != nil {
   114→		return "", err
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	return filepath.Join(root, relPath), nil
   118→}
   119→
   120→// IsInfrastructurePath returns true if the path is part of CLI infrastructure
   121→// (i.e., inside the .entire directory)
   122→func IsInfrastructurePath(path string) bool {
   123→	return strings.HasPrefix(path, EntireDir+"/") || path == EntireDir
   124→}
   125→
   126→// ToRelativePath converts an absolute path to relative.
   127→// Returns empty string if the path is outside the working directory.
   128→func ToRelativePath(absPath, cwd string) string {
   129→	if !filepath.IsAbs(absPath) {
   130→		return absPath
   131→	}
   132→	relPath, err := filepath.Rel(cwd, absPath)
   133→	if err != nil || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, "..") {
   134→		return ""
   135→	}
   136→	return relPath
   137→}
   138→
   139→// nonAlphanumericRegex matches any non-alphanumeric character
   140→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   141→
   142→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   143→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   144→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   145→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   146→}
   147→
   148→// GetClaudeProjectDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts
   149→// for the given repository path.
   150→//
   151→// In test environments, set ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR to override the default location.
   152→func GetClaudeProjectDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   153→	override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR")
   154→	if override != "" {
   155→		return override, nil
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   159→	if err != nil {
   160→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   164→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   165→}
   166→
   167→// SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID returns the path to a session's metadata directory
   168→// for the given Entire session ID. The sessionID must be the full, already date-prefixed
   169→// Entire session identifier as stored on disk, not an agent-specific or raw Claude ID.
   170→func SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID string) string {
   171→	return EntireMetadataDir + "/" + sessionID
   172→}
   173→
   174→// ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath attempts to extract a session ID from a transcript path.
   175→// Claude transcripts are stored at ~/.claude/projects/<project>/sessions/<id>.jsonl
   176→// If the path doesn't match expected format, returns empty string.
   177→func ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(transcriptPath string) string {
   178→	// Try to extract from typical path: ~/.claude/projects/<project>/sessions/<id>.jsonl
   179→	parts := strings.Split(filepath.ToSlash(transcriptPath), "/")
   180→	for i, part := range parts {
   181→		if part == "sessions" && i+1 < len(parts) {
   182→			// Return filename without extension
   183→			filename := parts[i+1]
   184→			if strings.HasSuffix(filename, ".jsonl") {
   185→				return strings.TrimSuffix(filename, ".jsonl")
   186→			}
   187→			return filename
   188→		}
   189→	}
   190→	return ""
   191→}

     1→// Package id provides the CheckpointID type for identifying checkpoints.
     2→// This is a separate package to avoid import cycles between paths, trailers, and checkpoint.
     3→package id
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"crypto/rand"
     7→	"encoding/hex"
     8→	"encoding/json"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"regexp"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// CheckpointID is a 12-character hex identifier for checkpoints.
    14→// It's used to link code commits to metadata on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
    15→//
    16→//nolint:recvcheck // UnmarshalJSON requires pointer receiver, others use value receiver - standard pattern
    17→type CheckpointID string
    18→
    19→// EmptyCheckpointID represents an unset or invalid checkpoint ID.
    20→const EmptyCheckpointID CheckpointID = ""
    21→
    22→// Pattern is the regex pattern for a valid checkpoint ID: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
    23→// Exported for use in other packages (e.g., trailers) to avoid pattern duplication.
    24→const Pattern = `[0-9a-f]{12}`
    25→
    26→// ShortIDLength is the standard length for truncating IDs for display purposes.
    27→// Used for tool use IDs, session IDs, and commit hashes in logs and messages.
    28→const ShortIDLength = 12
    29→
    30→// checkpointIDRegex validates the format: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
    31→var checkpointIDRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^` + Pattern + `$`)
    32→
    33→// NewCheckpointID creates a CheckpointID from a string, validating its format.
    34→// Returns an error if the string is not a valid 12-character hex ID.
    35→func NewCheckpointID(s string) (CheckpointID, error) {
    36→	if err := Validate(s); err != nil {
    37→		return EmptyCheckpointID, err
    38→	}
    39→	return CheckpointID(s), nil
    40→}
    41→
    42→// MustCheckpointID creates a CheckpointID from a string, panicking if invalid.
    43→// Use only when the ID is known to be valid (e.g., from trusted sources).
    44→func MustCheckpointID(s string) CheckpointID {
    45→	id, err := NewCheckpointID(s)
    46→	if err != nil {
    47→		panic(err)
    48→	}
    49→	return id
    50→}
    51→
    52→// Generate creates a new random 12-character hex checkpoint ID.
    53→func Generate() (CheckpointID, error) {
    54→	bytes := make([]byte, 6) // 6 bytes = 12 hex chars
    55→	if _, err := rand.Read(bytes); err != nil {
    56→		return EmptyCheckpointID, fmt.Errorf("failed to generate random checkpoint ID: %w", err)
    57→	}
    58→	return CheckpointID(hex.EncodeToString(bytes)), nil
    59→}
    60→
    61→// Validate checks if a string is a valid checkpoint ID format.
    62→// Returns an error if invalid, nil if valid.
    63→func Validate(s string) error {
    64→	if !checkpointIDRegex.MatchString(s) {
    65→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters", s)
    66→	}
    67→	return nil
    68→}
    69→
    70→// String returns the checkpoint ID as a string.
    71→func (id CheckpointID) String() string {
    72→	return string(id)
    73→}
    74→
    75→// IsEmpty returns true if the checkpoint ID is empty or unset.
    76→func (id CheckpointID) IsEmpty() bool {
    77→	return id == EmptyCheckpointID
    78→}
    79→
    80→// Path returns the sharded path for this checkpoint ID on entire/checkpoints/v1.
    81→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
    82→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
    83→func (id CheckpointID) Path() string {
    84→	if len(id) < 3 {
    85→		return string(id)
    86→	}
    87→	return string(id[:2]) + "/" + string(id[2:])
    88→}
    89→
    90→// MarshalJSON implements json.Marshaler.
    91→func (id CheckpointID) MarshalJSON() ([]byte, error) {
    92→	data, err := json.Marshal(string(id))
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal checkpoint ID: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→	return data, nil
    97→}
    98→
    99→// UnmarshalJSON implements json.Unmarshaler with validation.
   100→// Returns an error if the JSON string is not a valid 12-character hex ID.
   101→// Empty strings are allowed and result in EmptyCheckpointID.
   102→func (id *CheckpointID) UnmarshalJSON(data []byte) error {
   103→	var s string
   104→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &s); err != nil {
   105→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   106→	}
   107→	// Allow empty strings (represents unset checkpoint ID)
   108→	if s == "" {
   109→		*id = EmptyCheckpointID
   110→		return nil
   111→	}
   112→	if err := Validate(s); err != nil {
   113→		return err
   114→	}
   115→	*id = CheckpointID(s)
   116→	return nil
   117→}
   118→

  1264→// ensureSessionsBranch ensures the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch exists.
  1265→func (s *GitStore) ensureSessionsBranch() error {
  1266→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1267→	_, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1268→	if err == nil {
  1269→		return nil // Branch exists
  1270→	}
  1271→
  1272→	// Create orphan branch with empty tree
  1273→	emptyTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, make(map[string]object.TreeEntry))
  1274→	if err != nil {
  1275→		return err
  1276→	}
  1277→
  1278→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
  1279→	commitHash, err := s.createCommit(emptyTreeHash, plumbing.ZeroHash, "Initialize sessions branch", authorName, authorEmail)
  1280→	if err != nil {
  1281→		return err
  1282→	}
  1283→
  1284→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
  1285→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
  1286→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
  1287→	}
  1288→	return nil
  1289→}
  1290→
  1291→// getSessionsBranchTree returns the tree object for the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
  1292→// Falls back to origin/entire/checkpoints/v1 if the local branch doesn't exist.
  1293→func (s *GitStore) getSessionsBranchTree() (*object.Tree, error) {
  1294→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1295→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1296→	if err != nil {
  1297→		// Local branch doesn't exist, try remote-tracking branch
  1298→		remoteRefName := plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1299→		ref, err = s.repo.Reference(remoteRefName, true)
  1300→		if err != nil {
  1301→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("sessions branch not found: %w", err)
  1302→		}
  1303→	}
  1304→
  1305→	commit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1306→	if err != nil {
  1307→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
  1308→	}
  1309→
  1310→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
  1311→	if err != nil {
  1312→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
  1313→	}
  1314→
  1315→	return tree, nil
  1316→}

  1318→// CreateBlobFromContent creates a blob object from in-memory content.
  1319→// Exported for use by strategy package (session_test.go)
  1320→func CreateBlobFromContent(repo *git.Repository, content []byte) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
  1321→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
  1322→	obj.SetType(plumbing.BlobObject)
  1323→	obj.SetSize(int64(len(content)))
  1324→
  1325→	writer, err := obj.Writer()
  1326→	if err != nil {
  1327→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get object writer: %w", err)
  1328→	}
  1329→
  1330→	_, err = writer.Write(content)
  1331→	if err != nil {
  1332→		_ = writer.Close()
  1333→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to write blob content: %w", err)
  1334→	}
  1335→	if err := writer.Close(); err != nil {
  1336→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to close blob writer: %w", err)
  1337→	}
  1338→
  1339→	hash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
  1340→	if err != nil {
  1341→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to store blob object: %w", err)
  1342→	}
  1343→	return hash, nil
  1344→}
  1345→
  1346→// copyMetadataDir copies all files from a directory to the checkpoint path.
  1347→// Used to include additional metadata files like task checkpoints, subagent transcripts, etc.
  1348→func (s *GitStore) copyMetadataDir(metadataDir, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
  1349→	err := filepath.Walk(metadataDir, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, err error) error {
  1350→		if err != nil {
  1351→			return err
  1352→		}
  1353→
  1354→		// Skip symlinks to prevent reading files outside the metadata directory.
  1355→		// A symlink could point to sensitive files (e.g., /etc/passwd) which would
  1356→		// then be captured in the checkpoint and stored in git history.
  1357→		// NOTE: filepath.Walk uses os.Stat (follows symlinks), so info.Mode() never
  1358→		// reports ModeSymlink. We use os.Lstat to check the entry itself.
  1359→		// This check MUST come before IsDir() because Walk follows symlinked
  1360→		// directories and would recurse into them otherwise.
  1361→		linfo, lstatErr := os.Lstat(path)
  1362→		if lstatErr != nil {
  1363→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to lstat %s: %w", path, lstatErr)
  1364→		}
  1365→		if linfo.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink != 0 {
  1366→			if info.IsDir() {
  1367→				return filepath.SkipDir
  1368→			}
  1369→			return nil
  1370→		}
  1371→
  1372→		if info.IsDir() {
  1373→			return nil
  1374→		}
  1375→
  1376→		// Get relative path within metadata dir
  1377→		relPath, err := filepath.Rel(metadataDir, path)
  1378→		if err != nil {
  1379→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to get relative path for %s: %w", path, err)
  1380→		}
  1381→
  1382→		// Prevent path traversal via symlinks pointing outside the metadata dir
  1383→		if strings.HasPrefix(relPath, "..") {
  1384→			return fmt.Errorf("path traversal detected: %s", relPath)
  1385→		}
  1386→
  1387→		// Create blob from file with secrets redaction
  1388→		blobHash, mode, err := createRedactedBlobFromFile(s.repo, path, relPath)
  1389→		if err != nil {
  1390→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob for %s: %w", path, err)
  1391→		}
  1392→
  1393→		// Store at checkpoint path (use forward slashes for git tree compatibility on Windows)
  1394→		fullPath := basePath + filepath.ToSlash(relPath)
  1395→		entries[fullPath] = object.TreeEntry{
  1396→			Name: fullPath,
  1397→			Mode: mode,
  1398→			Hash: blobHash,
  1399→		}
  1400→
  1401→		return nil
  1402→	})
  1403→	if err != nil {
  1404→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to walk metadata directory: %w", err)
  1405→	}
  1406→	return nil
  1407→}
  1408→
  1409→// createRedactedBlobFromFile reads a file, applies secrets redaction, and creates a git blob.
  1410→// JSONL files get JSONL-aware redaction; all other files get plain string redaction.
  1411→func createRedactedBlobFromFile(repo *git.Repository, filePath, treePath string) (plumbing.Hash, filemode.FileMode, error) {
  1412→	info, err := os.Stat(filePath)
  1413→	if err != nil {
  1414→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to stat file: %w", err)
  1415→	}
  1416→
  1417→	mode := filemode.Regular
  1418→	if info.Mode()&0o111 != 0 {
  1419→		mode = filemode.Executable
  1420→	}
  1421→
  1422→	content, err := os.ReadFile(filePath) //nolint:gosec // filePath comes from walking the metadata directory
  1423→	if err != nil {
  1424→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read file: %w", err)
  1425→	}
  1426→
  1427→	// Skip redaction for binary files — they can't contain text secrets and
  1428→	// running string replacement on them would corrupt the data.
  1429→	isBin, binErr := binary.IsBinary(bytes.NewReader(content))
  1430→	if binErr != nil || isBin {
  1431→		hash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(repo, content)
  1432→		if err != nil {
  1433→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob: %w", err)
  1434→		}
  1435→		return hash, mode, nil
  1436→	}
  1437→
  1438→	if strings.HasSuffix(treePath, ".jsonl") {
  1439→		redacted, jsonlErr := redact.JSONLBytes(content)
  1440→		if jsonlErr != nil {
  1441→			redacted = redact.Bytes(content)
  1442→		}
  1443→		content = redacted
  1444→	} else {
  1445→		content = redact.Bytes(content)
  1446→	}
  1447→
  1448→	hash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(repo, content)
  1449→	if err != nil {
  1450→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob: %w", err)
  1451→	}
  1452→	return hash, mode, nil
  1453→}

  1455→// GetGitAuthorFromRepo retrieves the git user.name and user.email,
  1456→// checking both the repository-local config and the global ~/.gitconfig.
  1457→func GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo *git.Repository) (name, email string) {
  1458→	// Get repository config (includes local settings)
  1459→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
  1460→	if err == nil {
  1461→		name = cfg.User.Name
  1462→		email = cfg.User.Email
  1463→	}
  1464→
  1465→	// If not found in local config, try global config
  1466→	if name == "" || email == "" {
  1467→		globalCfg, err := config.LoadConfig(config.GlobalScope)
  1468→		if err == nil {
  1469→			if name == "" {
  1470→				name = globalCfg.User.Name
  1471→			}
  1472→			if email == "" {
  1473→				email = globalCfg.User.Email
  1474→			}
  1475→		}
  1476→	}
  1477→
  1478→	// Provide sensible defaults if git user is not configured
  1479→	if name == "" {
  1480→		name = "Unknown"
  1481→	}
  1482→	if email == "" {
  1483→		email = "unknown@local"
  1484→	}
  1485→
  1486→	return name, email
  1487→}

    44→// HashWorktreeID returns a short hash of the worktree identifier.
    45→// Used to create unique shadow branch names per worktree.
    46→func HashWorktreeID(worktreeID string) string {
    47→	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(worktreeID))
    48→	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])[:WorktreeIDHashLength]

    51→// WriteTemporary writes a temporary checkpoint to a shadow branch.
    52→// Shadow branches are named entire/<base-commit-short-hash>.

    55→// is skipped to avoid duplicate commits (deduplication).
    56→func (s *GitStore) WriteTemporary(ctx context.Context, opts WriteTemporaryOptions) (WriteTemporaryResult, error) {

    58→	if opts.BaseCommit == "" {

    66→

    68→	shadowBranchName := ShadowBranchNameForCommit(opts.BaseCommit, opts.WorktreeID)
    69→

    74→	}

    78→	if parentHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {
    79→		if lastCommit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(parentHash); err == nil {
    80→			lastTreeHash = lastCommit.TreeHash

    29→func NewRootCmd() *cobra.Command {

    32→		Short: "Entire CLI",

    41→		PersistentPostRun: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) {
    42→			// Skip for hidden commands (walk parent chain — Cobra doesn't propagate Hidden)
    43→			for c := cmd; c != nil; c = c.Parent() {
    44→				if c.Hidden {
    45→					return
    46→				}
    47→			}

     1→// lifecycle.go implements the generic lifecycle event dispatcher.
     2→// It routes normalized events from any agent to the appropriate framework actions.
     3→//
     4→// The dispatcher inverts the current flow from "agent handler calls framework functions"
     5→// to "framework dispatcher calls agent methods." Agents are passive data providers;
     6→// the dispatcher handles all orchestration: state transitions, strategy calls,
     7→// file change detection, metadata generation.
     8→package cli
     9→
    10→import (
    11→	"context"
    12→	"errors"
    13→	"fmt"
    14→	"log/slog"
    15→	"os"
    16→	"path/filepath"
    17→	"strings"
    18→	"time"
    19→
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    21→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    22→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    23→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    24→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    25→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    26→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/validation"
    27→)
    28→
    29→// DispatchLifecycleEvent routes a normalized lifecycle event to the appropriate handler.
    30→// Returns nil if the event was handled successfully.
    31→func DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    32→	if ag == nil {
    33→		return errors.New("agent cannot be nil")
    34→	}
    35→	if event == nil {
    36→		return errors.New("event cannot be nil")
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	switch event.Type {
    40→	case agent.SessionStart:
    41→		return handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag, event)
    42→	case agent.TurnStart:
    43→		return handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag, event)
    44→	case agent.TurnEnd:
    45→		return handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event)
    46→	case agent.Compaction:
    47→		return handleLifecycleCompaction(ag, event)
    48→	case agent.SessionEnd:
    49→		return handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag, event)
    50→	case agent.SubagentStart:
    51→		return handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag, event)
    52→	case agent.SubagentEnd:
    53→		return handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag, event)
    54→	default:
    55→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown lifecycle event type: %d", event.Type)
    56→	}
    57→}
    58→
    59→// handleLifecycleSessionStart handles session start: shows banner, checks concurrent sessions,
    60→// fires state machine transition.
    61→func handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    62→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
    63→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
    64→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
    65→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
    66→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
    67→	)
    68→
    69→	if event.SessionID == "" {
    70→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
    71→	}
    72→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(event.SessionID); err != nil {
    73→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	// Build informational message
    77→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
    78→
    79→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
    80→	strat := GetStrategy()
    81→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
    82→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(event.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
    83→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Output informational message
    88→	if event.ResponseMessage != "" {
    89→		message = event.ResponseMessage
    90→	}
    91→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
    92→		return err
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
    96→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
    97→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
    98→	} else if state != nil {
    99→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   100→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   101→		}
   102→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   103→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   104→		}
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	return nil
   108→}
   109→
   110→// handleLifecycleTurnStart handles turn start: captures pre-prompt state,
   111→// ensures strategy setup, initializes session.
   112→func handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   113→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   114→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-start",
   115→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   116→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   117→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   118→	)
   119→
   120→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   121→	if sessionID == "" {
   122→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
   123→	}
   124→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   125→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	// Capture pre-prompt state (including transcript position via TranscriptAnalyzer)
   129→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(ag, sessionID, event.SessionRef); err != nil {
   130→		return err
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	// Ensure strategy setup and initialize session
   134→	strat := GetStrategy()
   135→
   136→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   137→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   141→		agentType := ag.Type()
   142→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(sessionID, agentType, event.SessionRef, event.Prompt); err != nil {
   143→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   144→		}
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	return nil
   148→}
   149→
   150→// handleLifecycleTurnEnd handles turn end: validates transcript, extracts metadata,
   151→// detects file changes, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase.
   152→//
   153→//nolint:maintidx // high complexity due to sequential orchestration of 8 steps (validation, extraction, file detection, filtering, token calc, step save, phase transition, cleanup) - splitting would obscure the flow
   154→func handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   155→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   156→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-end",
   157→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   158→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   159→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   160→	)
   161→
   162→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   163→	if sessionID == "" {
   164→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	transcriptRef := event.SessionRef
   168→	if transcriptRef == "" {
   169→		return errors.New("transcript file not specified")
   170→	}
   171→	if !fileExists(transcriptRef) {
   172→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found: %s", transcriptRef)
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   176→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   177→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   178→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Create session metadata directory
   182→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   183→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir
   186→	}
   187→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   188→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// If agent implements TranscriptPreparer, wait for transcript to be ready
   192→	if preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {
   193→		if err := preparer.PrepareTranscript(transcriptRef); err != nil {
   194→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to prepare transcript: %v\n", err)
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Copy transcript to session directory
   199→	transcriptData, err := ag.ReadTranscript(transcriptRef)
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   204→	if err := os.WriteFile(logFile, transcriptData, 0o600); err != nil {
   205→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   208→
   209→	// Copy export JSON if it exists alongside the transcript (written by OpenCode plugin).
   210→	// This is used by `opencode import` during resume/rewind to restore the session
   211→	// into OpenCode's SQLite database with the original session ID.
   212→	exportSrc := strings.TrimSuffix(transcriptRef, filepath.Ext(transcriptRef)) + ".export.json"
   213→	if exportData, readErr := os.ReadFile(exportSrc); readErr == nil { //nolint:gosec // Path derived from agent hook
   214→		exportDest := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ExportDataFileName)
   215→		if writeErr := os.WriteFile(exportDest, exportData, 0o600); writeErr != nil {
   216→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to write export data: %v\n", writeErr)
   217→		}
   218→	}
   219→
   220→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on TurnStart)
   221→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   222→	if err != nil {
   223→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   224→	}
   225→
   226→	// Determine transcript offset
   227→	transcriptOffset := resolveTranscriptOffset(preState, sessionID)
   228→
   229→	// Extract metadata via agent interface (prompts, summary, modified files)
   230→	var allPrompts []string
   231→	var summary string
   232→	var modifiedFiles []string
   233→	var newTranscriptPosition int
   234→
   235→	// Compute subagents directory for agents that support subagent extraction.
   236→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/
   237→	subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptRef), event.SessionID, "subagents")
   238→
   239→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   240→		// Extract prompts
   241→		if prompts, promptErr := analyzer.ExtractPrompts(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); promptErr != nil {
   242→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\n", promptErr)
   243→		} else {
   244→			allPrompts = prompts
   245→		}
   246→
   247→		// Extract summary
   248→		if s, sumErr := analyzer.ExtractSummary(transcriptRef); sumErr != nil {
   249→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\n", sumErr)
   250→		} else {
   251→			summary = s
   252→		}
   253→
   254→		// Extract modified files - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor if available to include subagent files
   255→		if subagentExtractor, subOk := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); subOk {
   256→			if files, fileErr := subagentExtractor.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir); fileErr != nil {
   257→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files (with subagents): %v\n", fileErr)
   258→			} else {
   259→				modifiedFiles = files
   260→			}
   261→			// Get position from basic analyzer
   262→			if _, pos, posErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); posErr == nil {
   263→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   264→			}
   265→		} else {
   266→			// Fall back to basic extraction (main transcript only)
   267→			if files, pos, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); fileErr != nil {
   268→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\n", fileErr)
   269→			} else {
   270→				modifiedFiles = files
   271→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   272→			}
   273→		}
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Write prompts file
   277→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   278→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   279→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   280→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   281→	}
   282→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   283→
   284→	// Write summary file
   285→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   286→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   287→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   288→	}
   289→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   290→
   291→	// Generate commit message from last prompt
   292→	lastPrompt := ""
   293→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   294→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   295→	}
   296→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   297→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   298→
   299→	// Get repo root for path normalization
   300→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   301→	if err != nil {
   302→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   303→	}
   304→
   305→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   306→	if preState != nil {
   307→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   308→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	// Detect file changes via git status
   312→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   313→	if err != nil {
   314→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	// Filter and normalize all paths
   318→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   319→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   320→	if changes != nil {
   321→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   322→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   323→	}
   324→
   325→	// Filter transcript-extracted files to exclude files already committed to HEAD.
   326→	// When an agent commits files mid-turn, those files are condensed by PostCommit
   327→	// and should not be re-added to FilesTouched by SaveStep. A file is "committed"
   328→	// if it exists in HEAD with the same content as the working tree.
   329→	relModifiedFiles = filterToUncommittedFiles(relModifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   330→
   331→	// Check if there are any changes
   332→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   333→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   334→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   335→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   336→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   337→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   338→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   339→		}
   340→		return nil
   341→	}
   342→
   343→	// Log file changes
   344→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   345→
   346→	// Create context file
   347→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   348→	if err := createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, allPrompts, summary); err != nil {
   349→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   350→	}
   351→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   352→
   353→	// Get git author
   354→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   355→	if err != nil {
   356→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   357→	}
   358→
   359→	// Get strategy and agent type
   360→	strat := GetStrategy()
   361→	agentType := ag.Type()
   362→
   363→	// Get transcript position/identifier from pre-prompt state
   364→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   365→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   366→	if preState != nil {
   367→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   368→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.TranscriptOffset
   369→	}
   370→
   371→	// Calculate token usage - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor to include subagent tokens
   372→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   373→	if subagentExtractor, ok := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); ok {
   374→		usage, tokenErr := subagentExtractor.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   375→		if tokenErr != nil {
   376→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage (with subagents): %v\n", tokenErr)
   377→		} else {
   378→			tokenUsage = usage
   379→		}
   380→	} else if calculator, ok := ag.(agent.TokenCalculator); ok {
   381→		// Fall back to basic token calculation (main transcript only)
   382→		usage, tokenErr := calculator.CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart)
   383→		if tokenErr != nil {
   384→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   385→		} else {
   386→			tokenUsage = usage
   387→		}
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	// Build fully-populated step context and delegate to strategy
   391→	ctx := strategy.StepContext{
   392→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   393→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   394→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   395→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   396→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   397→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   398→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   399→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptRef,
   400→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   401→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   402→		AgentType:                agentType,
   403→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   404→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   405→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   406→	}
   407→
   408→	if err := strat.SaveStep(ctx); err != nil {
   409→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save step: %w", err)
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	// Update session state transcript position for auto-commit strategy
   413→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && newTranscriptPosition > 0 {
   414→		updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID, newTranscriptPosition)
   415→	}
   416→
   417→	// Transition session phase and cleanup
   418→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   419→	if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   420→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   421→	}
   422→
   423→	return nil
   424→}
   425→
   426→// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves current progress
   427→// but stays in ACTIVE phase (unlike TurnEnd which transitions to IDLE).
   428→// Also resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.
   429→func handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   430→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   431→	logging.Info(logCtx, "compaction",
   432→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   433→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   434→	)
   435→
   436→	// Fire EventCompaction to trigger ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched (stays in ACTIVE)
   437→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   438→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   439→	if loadErr != nil {
   440→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for compaction: %v\n", loadErr)
   441→	}
   442→	if sessionState != nil {
   443→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(sessionState, session.EventCompaction, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   444→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: compaction transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   445→		}
   446→
   447→		// Reset transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated/reorganized
   448→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
   449→
   450→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {
   451→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save session state after compaction: %v\n", saveErr)
   452→		}
   453→	}
   454→
   455→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Context compaction: transcript offset reset\n")
   456→	return nil
   457→}
   458→
   459→// handleLifecycleSessionEnd handles session end: marks the session as ended.
   460→func handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   461→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   462→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   463→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   464→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   465→	)
   466→
   467→	if event.SessionID == "" {
   468→		return nil // No session to update
   469→	}
   470→
   471→	if err := markSessionEnded(event.SessionID); err != nil {
   472→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   473→	}
   474→	return nil
   475→}
   476→
   477→// handleLifecycleSubagentStart handles subagent start: captures pre-task state.
   478→func handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   479→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   480→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-start",
   481→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   482→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   483→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   484→	)
   485→
   486→	// Log context
   487→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent started\n")
   488→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   489→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   490→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Transcript: %s\n", event.SessionRef)
   491→
   492→	// Capture pre-task state
   493→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(event.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   494→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   495→	}
   496→
   497→	return nil
   498→}
   499→
   500→// handleLifecycleSubagentEnd handles subagent end: detects changes, saves task checkpoint.
   501→func handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   502→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   503→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-end",
   504→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   505→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   506→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   507→		slog.String("subagent_id", event.SubagentID),
   508→	)
   509→
   510→	// Extract subagent type and description from tool input
   511→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(event.ToolInput)
   512→
   513→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   514→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(event.SessionRef)
   515→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   516→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   517→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, event.SubagentID)
   518→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   519→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   520→		}
   521→	}
   522→
   523→	// Log context
   524→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent completed\n")
   525→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   526→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   527→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   528→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Agent ID: %s\n", event.SubagentID)
   529→	}
   530→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   531→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Subagent Transcript: %s\n", subagentTranscriptPath)
   532→	}
   533→
   534→	// Extract modified files from subagent transcript
   535→	var modifiedFiles []string
   536→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   537→		transcriptToScan := event.SessionRef
   538→		if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   539→			transcriptToScan = subagentTranscriptPath
   540→		}
   541→		if files, _, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptToScan, 0); fileErr != nil {
   542→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files from subagent: %v\n", fileErr)
   543→		} else {
   544→			modifiedFiles = files
   545→		}
   546→	}
   547→
   548→	// Load pre-task state and detect file changes
   549→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID)
   550→	if err != nil {
   551→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   552→	}
   553→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   554→	if preState != nil {
   555→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   556→	}
   557→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   558→	if err != nil {
   559→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   560→	}
   561→
   562→	// Get repo root and normalize paths
   563→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   564→	if err != nil {
   565→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   566→	}
   567→
   568→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   569→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   570→	if changes != nil {
   571→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   572→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   573→	}
   574→
   575→	// If no changes, skip
   576→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   577→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   578→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   579→		return nil
   580→	}
   581→
   582→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort)
   583→	var checkpointUUID string
   584→	// Use the existing CLI-level checkpoint UUID finder
   585→	mainLines, _, _ := parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(event.SessionRef) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort
   586→	if mainLines != nil {
   587→		checkpointUUID, _ = FindCheckpointUUID(mainLines, event.ToolUseID)
   588→	}
   589→
   590→	// Get git author
   591→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   592→	if err != nil {
   593→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   594→	}
   595→
   596→	// Build task checkpoint context
   597→	strat := GetStrategy()
   598→	agentType := ag.Type()
   599→
   600→	ctx := strategy.TaskStepContext{
   601→		SessionID:              event.SessionID,
   602→		ToolUseID:              event.ToolUseID,
   603→		AgentID:                event.SubagentID,
   604→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   605→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   606→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   607→		TranscriptPath:         event.SessionRef,
   608→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   609→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   610→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   611→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   612→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   613→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   614→		AgentType:              agentType,
   615→	}
   616→
   617→	if err := strat.SaveTaskStep(ctx); err != nil {
   618→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task step: %w", err)
   619→	}
   620→
   621→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   622→	return nil
   623→}
   624→
   625→// --- Helper functions ---
   626→
   627→// resolveTranscriptOffset determines the transcript offset to use for parsing.
   628→// Prefers pre-prompt state, falls back to session state.
   629→func resolveTranscriptOffset(preState *PrePromptState, sessionID string) int {
   630→	if preState != nil && preState.TranscriptOffset > 0 {
   631→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", preState.TranscriptOffset)
   632→		return preState.TranscriptOffset
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   636→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   637→	if loadErr != nil {
   638→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   639→		return 0
   640→	}
   641→	if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   642→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   643→		return sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   644→	}
   645→
   646→	return 0
   647→}
   648→
   649→// updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition updates the session state with the new transcript position
   650→// for the auto-commit strategy.
   651→func updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID string, newPosition int) {
   652→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   653→	if loadErr != nil {
   654→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   655→		return
   656→	}
   657→	if sessionState == nil {
   658→		sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   659→			SessionID: sessionID,
   660→		}
   661→	}
   662→	sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = newPosition
   663→	sessionState.StepCount++
   664→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   665→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   666→	} else {
   667→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   668→			newPosition, sessionState.StepCount)
   669→	}
   670→}
   671→
   672→// createContextFile creates a context.md file for the session checkpoint.
   673→// This is a unified version that works for all agents.
   674→func createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   675→	var sb strings.Builder
   676→
   677→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   678→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Session ID: %s\n", sessionID)
   679→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage)
   680→
   681→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   682→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   683→		for i, p := range prompts {
   684→			fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p)
   685→		}
   686→	}
   687→
   688→	if summary != "" {
   689→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   690→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   691→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   692→	}
   693→
   694→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   695→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   696→	}
   697→	return nil
   698→}
   699→
   700→// parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID is a thin wrapper around transcript parsing for checkpoint UUID lookup.
   701→// Returns parsed transcript lines for use with FindCheckpointUUID.
   702→func parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(transcriptPath string) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {
   703→	lines, total, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   704→	if err != nil {
   705→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing transcript for checkpoint UUID: %w", err)
   706→	}
   707→	return lines, total, nil
   708→}
   709→
   710→// transitionSessionTurnEnd transitions the session phase to IDLE and dispatches turn-end actions.
   711→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   712→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   713→	if loadErr != nil {
   714→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   715→		return
   716→	}
   717→	if turnState == nil {
   718→		return
   719→	}
   720→	if err := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); err != nil {
   721→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end transition failed: %v\n", err)
   722→	}
   723→
   724→	// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads
   725→	// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.
   726→	strat := GetStrategy()
   727→	if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   728→		if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {
   729→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   730→		}
   731→	}
   732→
   733→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   734→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   735→	}
   736→}
   737→
   738→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   739→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   740→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   741→	if err != nil {
   742→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   743→	}
   744→	if state == nil {
   745→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   746→	}
   747→
   748→	if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   749→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session stop transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   750→	}
   751→
   752→	now := time.Now()
   753→	state.EndedAt = &now
   754→
   755→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   756→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   757→	}
   758→	return nil
   759→}
   760→
   761→// logFileChanges logs the files modified, created, and deleted during a session.
   762→func logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {
   763→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(modified))
   764→	for _, file := range modified {
   765→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   766→	}
   767→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   768→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(newFiles))
   769→		for _, file := range newFiles {
   770→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   771→		}
   772→	}
   773→	if len(deleted) > 0 {
   774→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(deleted))
   775→		for _, file := range deleted {
   776→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   777→		}
   778→	}
   779→}

     1→package strategy
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"errors"

    19→
    20→// pushSessionsBranchCommon is the shared implementation for pushing session branches.
    21→// Used by both manual-commit and auto-commit strategies.
    22→// By default, session logs are pushed automatically alongside user pushes.
    23→// Configuration (stored in .entire/settings.json under strategy_options.push_sessions):
    24→//   - false: disable automatic pushing
    25→//   - true or not set: push automatically (default)
    26→func pushSessionsBranchCommon(remote, branchName string) error {
    27→	// Check if pushing is disabled
    28→	if isPushSessionsDisabled() {
    29→		return nil
    30→	}
    31→
    32→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    33→	if err != nil {
    34→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
    35→	}
    36→
    37→	// Check if branch exists locally
    38→	branchRef := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName)
    39→	localRef, err := repo.Reference(branchRef, true)
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		// No branch, nothing to push
    42→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Expected when no sessions exist yet
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	// Check if there's actually something to push (local differs from remote)
    46→	if !hasUnpushedSessionsCommon(repo, remote, localRef.Hash(), branchName) {
    47→		// Nothing to push - skip silently
    48→		return nil
    49→	}
    50→
    51→	return doPushSessionsBranch(remote, branchName)
    52→}
    53→
    54→// hasUnpushedSessionsCommon checks if the local branch differs from the remote.
    55→// Returns true if there's any difference that needs syncing (local ahead, remote ahead, or diverged).
    56→func hasUnpushedSessionsCommon(repo *git.Repository, remote string, localHash plumbing.Hash, branchName string) bool {
    57→	// Check for remote tracking ref: refs/remotes/<remote>/<branch>
    58→	remoteRefName := plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName(remote, branchName)
    59→	remoteRef, err := repo.Reference(remoteRefName, true)
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		// Remote branch doesn't exist yet - we have content to push
    62→		return true
    63→	}
    64→
    65→	// If local and remote point to same commit, nothing to sync
    66→	// This is the only case where we skip - any difference needs handling
    67→	return localHash != remoteRef.Hash()
    68→}
    69→
    70→// isPushSessionsDisabled checks if push_sessions is disabled in settings.
    71→// Returns true if push_sessions is explicitly set to false.
    72→func isPushSessionsDisabled() bool {
    73→	s, err := settings.Load()
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		return false // Default: push is enabled
    76→	}
    77→	return s.IsPushSessionsDisabled()
    78→}
    79→
    80→// doPushSessionsBranch pushes the sessions branch to the remote.
    81→func doPushSessionsBranch(remote, branchName string) error {
    82→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Pushing session logs to %s...\n", remote)
    83→
    84→	// Try pushing first
    85→	if err := tryPushSessionsCommon(remote, branchName); err == nil {
    86→		return nil
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	// Push failed - likely non-fast-forward. Try to fetch and merge.
    90→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Syncing with remote session logs...\n")
    91→
    92→	if err := fetchAndMergeSessionsCommon(remote, branchName); err != nil {
    93→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: couldn't sync sessions: %v\n", err)
    94→		return nil // Don't fail the main push
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	// Try pushing again after merge
    98→	if err := tryPushSessionsCommon(remote, branchName); err != nil {
    99→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to push sessions after sync: %v\n", err)
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	return nil
   103→}
   104→
   105→// tryPushSessionsCommon attempts to push the sessions branch.
   106→func tryPushSessionsCommon(remote, branchName string) error {
   107→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Minute)
   108→	defer cancel()
   109→
   110→	// Use --no-verify to prevent recursive hook calls
   111→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "push", "--no-verify", remote, branchName)
   112→	cmd.Stdin = nil // Disconnect stdin to prevent hanging in hook context
   113→
   114→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		// Check if it's a non-fast-forward error (we can try to recover)
   117→		if strings.Contains(string(output), "non-fast-forward") ||
   118→			strings.Contains(string(output), "rejected") {
   119→			return errors.New("non-fast-forward")
   120→		}
   121→		return fmt.Errorf("push failed: %s", output)
   122→	}
   123→	return nil
   124→}
   125→
   126→// fetchAndMergeSessionsCommon fetches remote sessions and merges into local using go-git.
   127→// Since session logs are append-only (unique cond-* directories), we just combine trees.
   128→func fetchAndMergeSessionsCommon(remote, branchName string) error {
   129→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Minute)
   130→	defer cancel()
   131→
   132→	// Use git CLI for fetch (go-git's fetch can be tricky with auth)
   133→	fetchCmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "fetch", remote, branchName)
   134→	fetchCmd.Stdin = nil
   135→	if output, err := fetchCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   136→		return fmt.Errorf("fetch failed: %s", output)
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   140→	if err != nil {
   141→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	// Get local branch
   145→	localRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), true)
   146→	if err != nil {
   147→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get local ref: %w", err)
   148→	}
   149→	localCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(localRef.Hash())
   150→	if err != nil {
   151→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get local commit: %w", err)
   152→	}
   153→	localTree, err := localCommit.Tree()
   154→	if err != nil {
   155→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get local tree: %w", err)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Get remote (FETCH_HEAD)
   159→	fetchHeadRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName("FETCH_HEAD"), true)
   160→	if err != nil {
   161→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get FETCH_HEAD: %w", err)
   162→	}
   163→	remoteCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(fetchHeadRef.Hash())
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get remote commit: %w", err)
   166→	}
   167→	remoteTree, err := remoteCommit.Tree()
   168→	if err != nil {
   169→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get remote tree: %w", err)
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	// Flatten both trees and combine entries
   173→	// Session logs have unique cond-* directories, so no conflicts expected
   174→	entries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)
   175→	if err := checkpoint.FlattenTree(repo, localTree, "", entries); err != nil {
   176→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to flatten local tree: %w", err)
   177→	}
   178→	if err := checkpoint.FlattenTree(repo, remoteTree, "", entries); err != nil {
   179→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to flatten remote tree: %w", err)
   180→	}
   181→
   182→	// Build merged tree
   183→	mergedTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(repo, entries)
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to build merged tree: %w", err)
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	// Create merge commit with both parents
   189→	mergeCommitHash, err := createMergeCommitCommon(repo, mergedTreeHash,
   190→		[]plumbing.Hash{localRef.Hash(), fetchHeadRef.Hash()},
   191→		"Merge remote session logs")
   192→	if err != nil {
   193→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create merge commit: %w", err)
   194→	}
   195→
   196→	// Update branch ref
   197→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), mergeCommitHash)
   198→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
   199→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update branch ref: %w", err)
   200→	}
   201→
   202→	return nil
   203→}
   204→
   205→// createMergeCommitCommon creates a merge commit with multiple parents.
   206→func createMergeCommitCommon(repo *git.Repository, treeHash plumbing.Hash, parents []plumbing.Hash, message string) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   207→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   208→	now := time.Now()
   209→	sig := object.Signature{
   210→		Name:  authorName,
   211→		Email: authorEmail,
   212→		When:  now,
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	commit := &object.Commit{
   216→		TreeHash:     treeHash,
   217→		ParentHashes: parents,
   218→		Author:       sig,
   219→		Committer:    sig,
   220→		Message:      message,
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   224→	if err := commit.Encode(obj); err != nil {
   225→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to encode commit: %w", err)
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	hash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
   229→	if err != nil {
   230→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to store commit: %w", err)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	return hash, nil
   234→}
   235→

    78→// IsOnDefaultBranch checks if the repository is currently on the default branch.
    79→// It determines the default branch by:
    80→// 1. Checking the remote origin's HEAD reference
    81→// 2. Falling back to common names (main, master) if remote HEAD is unavailable
    82→// Returns (isDefault, branchName, error)
    83→func IsOnDefaultBranch() (bool, string, error) {
    84→	repo, err := openRepository()
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	// Get current branch
    90→	head, err := repo.Head()
    91→	if err != nil {
    92→		return false, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	if !head.Name().IsBranch() {
    96→		// Detached HEAD - not on any branch
    97→		return false, "", nil
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	currentBranch := head.Name().Short()
   101→
   102→	// Try to get default branch from remote origin's HEAD
   103→	defaultBranch := getDefaultBranchFromRemote(repo)
   104→
   105→	// If we couldn't determine from remote, use common defaults
   106→	if defaultBranch == "" {
   107→		// Check if current branch is a common default name
   108→		if currentBranch == "main" || currentBranch == "master" {
   109→			return true, currentBranch, nil
   110→		}
   111→		return false, currentBranch, nil
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	return currentBranch == defaultBranch, currentBranch, nil
   115→}
   116→
   117→// getDefaultBranchFromRemote tries to determine the default branch from the origin remote.
   118→// Returns empty string if unable to determine.
   119→func getDefaultBranchFromRemote(repo *git.Repository) string {
   120→	// Try to get the symbolic reference for origin/HEAD
   121→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", "HEAD"), true)
   122→	if err == nil && ref != nil {
   123→		// ref.Target() gives us something like "refs/remotes/origin/main"
   124→		target := ref.Target().String()
   125→		if strings.HasPrefix(target, "refs/remotes/origin/") {
   126→			return strings.TrimPrefix(target, "refs/remotes/origin/")
   127→		}
   128→	}
   129→
   130→	// Fallback: check if origin/main or origin/master exists
   131→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", "main"), true); err == nil {
   132→		return "main"
   133→	}
   134→	if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", "master"), true); err == nil {
   135→		return "master"
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	return ""
   139→}
   140→
   141→// ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch checks if we're on the default branch.
   142→// Returns (shouldSkip, branchName). If shouldSkip is true, the caller should
   143→// skip the operation to avoid polluting main/master history.
   144→// If the branch cannot be determined, returns (false, "") to allow the operation.
   145→func ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch() (bool, string) {
   146→	isDefault, branchName, err := IsOnDefaultBranch()
   147→	if err != nil {
   148→		// If we can't determine, allow the operation
   149→		return false, ""
   150→	}
   151→	return isDefault, branchName
   152→}
   153→
   154→// GetCurrentBranch returns the name of the current branch.

   168→		return "", errors.New("not on a branch (detached HEAD)")
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	return head.Name().Short(), nil
   172→}

    10→// IsAccessibleMode returns true if accessibility mode should be enabled.
    11→// This checks the ACCESSIBLE environment variable.
    12→// Set ACCESSIBLE=1 (or any non-empty value) to enable accessible mode,
    13→// which uses simpler prompts that work better with screen readers.
    14→func IsAccessibleMode() bool {
    15→	return os.Getenv("ACCESSIBLE") != ""
    16→}
    17→
    18→// entireTheme returns the Dracula theme for consistent styling.
    19→func entireTheme() *huh.Theme {
    20→	return huh.ThemeDracula()
    21→}
    22→
    23→// NewAccessibleForm creates a new huh form with accessibility mode
    24→// enabled if the ACCESSIBLE environment variable is set.
    25→// Note: WithAccessible() is only available on forms, not individual fields.
    26→// Always wrap confirmations and other prompts in a form to enable accessibility.
    27→func NewAccessibleForm(groups ...*huh.Group) *huh.Form {
    28→	form := huh.NewForm(groups...).WithTheme(entireTheme())
    29→	if IsAccessibleMode() {
    30→		form = form.WithAccessible(true)
    31→	}
    32→	return form
    33→}
    34→
    35→// fileExists checks if a file exists
    36→func fileExists(path string) bool {

    38→	return err == nil
    39→}
    40→
    41→// copyFile copies a file from src to dst
    42→func copyFile(src, dst string) error {
    43→	input, err := os.ReadFile(src) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled git metadata path
    44→	if err != nil {
    45→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
    46→	}
    47→	if err := os.WriteFile(dst, input, 0o600); err != nil {
    48→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write file: %w", err)
    49→	}
    50→	return nil
    51→}
