     1→package strategy
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"log/slog"
     7→	"regexp"
     8→	"strings"
     9→	"time"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint"
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/id"
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    16→
    17→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing"
    18→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object"
    19→)
    20→
    21→const (
    22→	// sessionGracePeriod is the minimum age a session must have before it can be
    23→	// considered orphaned. This protects active sessions that haven't created
    24→	// their first checkpoint yet.
    25→	sessionGracePeriod = 10 * time.Minute
    26→)
    27→
    28→// CleanupType identifies the type of orphaned item.
    29→type CleanupType string
    30→
    31→const (
    32→	CleanupTypeShadowBranch CleanupType = "shadow-branch"
    33→	CleanupTypeSessionState CleanupType = "session-state"
    34→	CleanupTypeCheckpoint   CleanupType = "checkpoint"
    35→)
    36→
    37→// CleanupItem represents an orphaned item that can be cleaned up.
    38→type CleanupItem struct {
    39→	Type   CleanupType
    40→	ID     string // Branch name, session ID, or checkpoint ID
    41→	Reason string // Why this item is considered orphaned
    42→}
    43→
    44→// CleanupResult contains the results of a cleanup operation.
    45→type CleanupResult struct {
    46→	ShadowBranches    []string // Deleted shadow branches
    47→	SessionStates     []string // Deleted session state files
    48→	Checkpoints       []string // Deleted checkpoint metadata
    49→	FailedBranches    []string // Shadow branches that failed to delete
    50→	FailedStates      []string // Session states that failed to delete
    51→	FailedCheckpoints []string // Checkpoints that failed to delete
    52→}
    53→
    54→// shadowBranchPattern matches shadow branch names in both old and new formats:
    55→//   - Old format: entire/<commit[:7+]>
    56→//   - New format: entire/<commit[:7+]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>
    57→//
    58→// The pattern requires at least 7 hex characters for the commit, optionally followed
    59→// by a dash and exactly 6 hex characters for the worktree hash.
    60→var shadowBranchPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^entire/[0-9a-fA-F]{7,}(-[0-9a-fA-F]{6})?$`)
    61→
    62→// IsShadowBranch returns true if the branch name matches the shadow branch pattern.
    63→// Shadow branches have the format "entire/<commit-hash>-<worktree-hash>" where the
    64→// commit hash is at least 7 hex characters and worktree hash is 6 hex characters.
    65→// The "entire/checkpoints/v1" branch is NOT a shadow branch.
    66→func IsShadowBranch(branchName string) bool {
    67→	// Explicitly exclude entire/checkpoints/v1
    68→	if branchName == paths.MetadataBranchName {
    69→		return false
    70→	}
    71→	return shadowBranchPattern.MatchString(branchName)
    72→}
    73→
    74→// ListShadowBranches returns all shadow branches in the repository.
    75→// Shadow branches match the pattern "entire/<commit-hash>" (7+ hex chars).
    76→// The "entire/checkpoints/v1" branch is excluded as it stores permanent metadata.
    77→// Returns an empty slice (not nil) if no shadow branches exist.
    78→func ListShadowBranches() ([]string, error) {
    79→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	refs, err := repo.References()
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get references: %w", err)
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	var shadowBranches []string
    90→
    91→	err = refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
    92→		// Only look at branch references
    93→		if !ref.Name().IsBranch() {
    94→			return nil
    95→		}
    96→
    97→		// Extract branch name without refs/heads/ prefix
    98→		branchName := strings.TrimPrefix(ref.Name().String(), "refs/heads/")
    99→
   100→		if IsShadowBranch(branchName) {
   101→			shadowBranches = append(shadowBranches, branchName)
   102→		}
   103→		return nil
   104→	})
   105→	if err != nil {
   106→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to iterate references: %w", err)
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	// Ensure we return empty slice, not nil
   110→	if shadowBranches == nil {
   111→		shadowBranches = []string{}
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	return shadowBranches, nil
   115→}
   116→
   117→// DeleteShadowBranches deletes the specified branches from the repository.
   118→// Returns two slices: successfully deleted branches and branches that failed to delete.
   119→// Individual branch deletion failures do not stop the operation - all branches are attempted.
   120→func DeleteShadowBranches(branches []string) (deleted []string, failed []string, err error) { //nolint:unparam // already present in codebase
   121→	if len(branches) == 0 {
   122→		return []string{}, []string{}, nil
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	for _, branch := range branches {
   126→		// Use git CLI to delete branches because go-git v5's RemoveReference
   127→		// doesn't properly persist deletions with packed refs or worktrees
   128→		if err := DeleteBranchCLI(branch); err != nil {
   129→			failed = append(failed, branch)
   130→			continue
   131→		}
   132→
   133→		deleted = append(deleted, branch)
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	return deleted, failed, nil
   137→}
   138→
   139→// ListOrphanedSessionStates returns session state files that are orphaned.
   140→// A session state is orphaned if:
   141→//   - No checkpoints on entire/checkpoints/v1 reference this session ID
   142→//   - No shadow branch exists for the session's base commit
   143→//
   144→// This is strategy-agnostic as session states are shared by all strategies.
   145→func ListOrphanedSessionStates() ([]CleanupItem, error) {
   146→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   147→	if err != nil {
   148→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	// Get all session states
   152→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
   153→	if err != nil {
   154→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
   158→	if err != nil {
   159→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	if len(states) == 0 {
   163→		return []CleanupItem{}, nil
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	// Get all checkpoints to find which sessions have checkpoints
   167→	cpStore := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
   168→
   169→	sessionsWithCheckpoints := make(map[string]bool)
   170→	checkpoints, listErr := cpStore.ListCommitted(context.Background())
   171→	if listErr == nil {
   172→		for _, cp := range checkpoints {
   173→			sessionsWithCheckpoints[cp.SessionID] = true
   174→		}
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	// Get all shadow branches as a set for quick lookup
   178→	shadowBranches, _ := ListShadowBranches() //nolint:errcheck // Best effort
   179→	shadowBranchSet := make(map[string]bool)
   180→	for _, branch := range shadowBranches {
   181→		shadowBranchSet[branch] = true
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	var orphaned []CleanupItem
   185→	now := time.Now()
   186→
   187→	for _, state := range states {
   188→		// Skip sessions that started recently - they may be actively in use
   189→		// but haven't created their first checkpoint yet
   190→		if now.Sub(state.StartedAt) < sessionGracePeriod {
   191→			continue
   192→		}
   193→
   194→		// Check if session has checkpoints on entire/checkpoints/v1
   195→		hasCheckpoints := sessionsWithCheckpoints[state.SessionID]
   196→
   197→		// Check if shadow branch exists for this session's base commit and worktree
   198→		// Shadow branches are now worktree-specific: entire/<commit[:7]>-<worktreeHash[:6]>
   199→		expectedBranch := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   200→		hasShadowBranch := shadowBranchSet[expectedBranch]
   201→
   202→		// Session is orphaned if it has no checkpoints AND no shadow branch
   203→		if !hasCheckpoints && !hasShadowBranch {
   204→			reason := "no checkpoints or shadow branch found"
   205→			orphaned = append(orphaned, CleanupItem{
   206→				Type:   CleanupTypeSessionState,
   207→				ID:     state.SessionID,
   208→				Reason: reason,
   209→			})
   210→		}
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	return orphaned, nil
   214→}
   215→
   216→// DeleteOrphanedSessionStates deletes the specified session state files.
   217→func DeleteOrphanedSessionStates(sessionIDs []string) (deleted []string, failed []string, err error) {
   218→	if len(sessionIDs) == 0 {
   219→		return []string{}, []string{}, nil
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
   223→	if err != nil {
   224→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create state store: %w", err)
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	for _, sessionID := range sessionIDs {
   228→		if err := store.Clear(context.Background(), sessionID); err != nil {
   229→			failed = append(failed, sessionID)
   230→		} else {
   231→			deleted = append(deleted, sessionID)
   232→		}
   233→	}
   234→
   235→	return deleted, failed, nil
   236→}
   237→
   238→// DeleteOrphanedCheckpoints removes checkpoint directories from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   239→func DeleteOrphanedCheckpoints(checkpointIDs []string) (deleted []string, failed []string, err error) {
   240→	if len(checkpointIDs) == 0 {
   241→		return []string{}, []string{}, nil
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   245→	if err != nil {
   246→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	// Get sessions branch
   250→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   251→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   252→	if err != nil {
   253→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("sessions branch not found: %w", err)
   254→	}
   255→
   256→	parentCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   257→	if err != nil {
   258→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
   259→	}
   260→
   261→	baseTree, err := parentCommit.Tree()
   262→	if err != nil {
   263→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	// Flatten tree to entries
   267→	entries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)
   268→	if err := checkpoint.FlattenTree(repo, baseTree, "", entries); err != nil {
   269→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to flatten tree: %w", err)
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	// Remove entries for each checkpoint
   273→	checkpointSet := make(map[string]bool)
   274→	for _, id := range checkpointIDs {
   275→		checkpointSet[id] = true
   276→	}
   277→
   278→	// Find and remove entries matching checkpoint paths
   279→	for path := range entries {
   280→		for checkpointIDStr := range checkpointSet {
   281→			cpID, err := id.NewCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
   282→			if err != nil {
   283→				continue // Skip invalid checkpoint IDs
   284→			}
   285→			cpPath := cpID.Path()
   286→			if strings.HasPrefix(path, cpPath+"/") {
   287→				delete(entries, path)
   288→			}
   289→		}
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	// Build new tree
   293→	newTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(repo, entries)
   294→	if err != nil {
   295→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to build tree: %w", err)
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	// Create commit
   299→	commit := &object.Commit{
   300→		Author: object.Signature{
   301→			Name:  "Entire CLI",
   302→			Email: "cli@entire.io",
   303→			When:  parentCommit.Author.When,
   304→		},
   305→		Committer: object.Signature{
   306→			Name:  "Entire CLI",
   307→			Email: "cli@entire.io",
   308→			When:  parentCommit.Committer.When,
   309→		},
   310→		Message:      fmt.Sprintf("Cleanup: removed %d orphaned checkpoints", len(checkpointIDs)),
   311→		TreeHash:     newTreeHash,
   312→		ParentHashes: []plumbing.Hash{ref.Hash()},
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
   316→	if err := commit.Encode(obj); err != nil {
   317→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to encode commit: %w", err)
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	commitHash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
   321→	if err != nil {
   322→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to store commit: %w", err)
   323→	}
   324→
   325→	// Update branch reference
   326→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
   327→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
   328→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to update branch: %w", err)
   329→	}
   330→
   331→	// All checkpoints deleted successfully
   332→	return checkpointIDs, []string{}, nil
   333→}
   334→
   335→// ListAllCleanupItems returns all orphaned items across all categories.
   336→// It iterates over all registered strategies and calls ListOrphanedItems on those
   337→// that implement OrphanedItemsLister.
   338→// Returns an error if the repository cannot be opened.
   339→func ListAllCleanupItems() ([]CleanupItem, error) {
   340→	var items []CleanupItem
   341→	var firstErr error
   342→
   343→	// Iterate over all registered strategies
   344→	for _, name := range List() {
   345→		strat, err := Get(name)
   346→		if err != nil {
   347→			if firstErr == nil {
   348→				firstErr = err
   349→			}
   350→			continue
   351→		}
   352→
   353→		// Check if strategy implements OrphanedItemsLister
   354→		if lister, ok := strat.(OrphanedItemsLister); ok {
   355→			stratItems, err := lister.ListOrphanedItems()
   356→			if err != nil {
   357→				if firstErr == nil {
   358→					firstErr = err
   359→				}
   360→				continue
   361→			}
   362→			items = append(items, stratItems...)
   363→		}
   364→	}
   365→
   366→	// Orphaned session states (strategy-agnostic)
   367→	states, err := ListOrphanedSessionStates()
   368→	if err != nil {
   369→		if firstErr == nil {
   370→			firstErr = err
   371→		}
   372→	} else {
   373→		items = append(items, states...)
   374→	}
   375→
   376→	return items, firstErr
   377→}
   378→
   379→// DeleteAllCleanupItems deletes all specified cleanup items.
   380→// Logs each deletion for audit purposes.
   381→func DeleteAllCleanupItems(items []CleanupItem) (*CleanupResult, error) {
   382→	result := &CleanupResult{}
   383→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "cleanup")
   384→
   385→	// Build ID-to-Reason map for logging after deletion
   386→	reasonMap := make(map[string]string)
   387→	for _, item := range items {
   388→		reasonMap[item.ID] = item.Reason
   389→	}
   390→
   391→	// Group items by type
   392→	var branches, states, checkpoints []string
   393→	for _, item := range items {
   394→		switch item.Type {
   395→		case CleanupTypeShadowBranch:
   396→			branches = append(branches, item.ID)
   397→		case CleanupTypeSessionState:
   398→			states = append(states, item.ID)
   399→		case CleanupTypeCheckpoint:
   400→			checkpoints = append(checkpoints, item.ID)
   401→		}
   402→	}
   403→
   404→	// Delete shadow branches
   405→	if len(branches) > 0 {
   406→		deleted, failed, err := DeleteShadowBranches(branches)
   407→		if err != nil {
   408→			return result, err
   409→		}
   410→		result.ShadowBranches = deleted
   411→		result.FailedBranches = failed
   412→
   413→		// Log deleted branches
   414→		for _, id := range deleted {
   415→			logging.Info(logCtx, "deleted orphaned shadow branch",
   416→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeShadowBranch)),
   417→				slog.String("id", id),
   418→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   419→			)
   420→		}
   421→		// Log failed branches
   422→		for _, id := range failed {
   423→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "failed to delete orphaned shadow branch",
   424→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeShadowBranch)),
   425→				slog.String("id", id),
   426→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   427→			)
   428→		}
   429→	}
   430→
   431→	// Delete session states
   432→	if len(states) > 0 {
   433→		deleted, failed, err := DeleteOrphanedSessionStates(states)
   434→		if err != nil {
   435→			return result, err
   436→		}
   437→		result.SessionStates = deleted
   438→		result.FailedStates = failed
   439→
   440→		// Log deleted session states
   441→		for _, id := range deleted {
   442→			logging.Info(logCtx, "deleted orphaned session state",
   443→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeSessionState)),
   444→				slog.String("id", id),
   445→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   446→			)
   447→		}
   448→		// Log failed session states
   449→		for _, id := range failed {
   450→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "failed to delete orphaned session state",
   451→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeSessionState)),
   452→				slog.String("id", id),
   453→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   454→			)
   455→		}
   456→	}
   457→
   458→	// Delete checkpoints
   459→	if len(checkpoints) > 0 {
   460→		deleted, failed, err := DeleteOrphanedCheckpoints(checkpoints)
   461→		if err != nil {
   462→			return result, err
   463→		}
   464→		result.Checkpoints = deleted
   465→		result.FailedCheckpoints = failed
   466→
   467→		// Log deleted checkpoints
   468→		for _, id := range deleted {
   469→			logging.Info(logCtx, "deleted orphaned checkpoint",
   470→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeCheckpoint)),
   471→				slog.String("id", id),
   472→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   473→			)
   474→		}
   475→		// Log failed checkpoints
   476→		for _, id := range failed {
   477→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "failed to delete orphaned checkpoint",
   478→				slog.String("type", string(CleanupTypeCheckpoint)),
   479→				slog.String("id", id),
   480→				slog.String("reason", reasonMap[id]),
   481→			)
   482→		}
   483→	}
   484→
   485→	// Log summary
   486→	totalDeleted := len(result.ShadowBranches) + len(result.SessionStates) + len(result.Checkpoints)
   487→	totalFailed := len(result.FailedBranches) + len(result.FailedStates) + len(result.FailedCheckpoints)
   488→	if totalDeleted > 0 || totalFailed > 0 {
   489→		logging.Info(logCtx, "cleanup completed",
   490→			slog.Int("deleted_branches", len(result.ShadowBranches)),
   491→			slog.Int("deleted_session_states", len(result.SessionStates)),
   492→			slog.Int("deleted_checkpoints", len(result.Checkpoints)),
   493→			slog.Int("failed_branches", len(result.FailedBranches)),
   494→			slog.Int("failed_session_states", len(result.FailedStates)),
   495→			slog.Int("failed_checkpoints", len(result.FailedCheckpoints)),
   496→		)
   497→	}
   498→
   499→	return result, nil
   500→}
   501→
```

    23→func newStatusCmd() *cobra.Command {
    24→	var detailed bool
    25→
    26→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    27→		Use:   "status",
    28→		Short: "Show Entire status",
    29→		Long:  "Show whether Entire is currently enabled or disabled",
    30→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    31→			return runStatus(cmd.OutOrStdout(), detailed)
    32→		},
    33→	}
    34→
    35→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&detailed, "detailed", false, "Show detailed status for each settings file")
    36→
    37→	return cmd
    38→}
    39→
    40→func runStatus(w io.Writer, detailed bool) error {
    41→	// Check if we're in a git repository
    42→	if _, repoErr := paths.RepoRoot(); repoErr != nil {
    43→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "✕ not a git repository")
    44→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Not being in a git repo is a valid status, not an error
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	// Get absolute paths for settings files
    48→	settingsPath, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsFile)
    49→	if err != nil {
    50→		settingsPath = EntireSettingsFile
    51→	}
    52→	localSettingsPath, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsLocalFile)
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		localSettingsPath = EntireSettingsLocalFile
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Check which settings files exist
    58→	_, projectErr := os.Stat(settingsPath)
    59→	if projectErr != nil && !errors.Is(projectErr, fs.ErrNotExist) {
    60→		return fmt.Errorf("cannot access project settings file: %w", projectErr)
    61→	}
    62→	_, localErr := os.Stat(localSettingsPath)
    63→	if localErr != nil && !errors.Is(localErr, fs.ErrNotExist) {
    64→		return fmt.Errorf("cannot access local settings file: %w", localErr)
    65→	}
    66→	projectExists := projectErr == nil
    67→	localExists := localErr == nil
    68→
    69→	if !projectExists && !localExists {
    70→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "○ not set up (run `entire enable` to get started)")
    71→		return nil
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	if detailed {
    75→		return runStatusDetailed(w, settingsPath, localSettingsPath, projectExists, localExists)
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	// Short output: just show the effective/merged state
    79→	settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	fmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatusShort(settings))
    85→
    86→	if settings.Enabled {
    87→		writeActiveSessions(w)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	return nil
    91→}
    92→
    93→// runStatusDetailed shows the effective status plus detailed status for each settings file.
    94→func runStatusDetailed(w io.Writer, settingsPath, localSettingsPath string, projectExists, localExists bool) error {
    95→	// First show the effective/merged status
    96→	effectiveSettings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
    97→	if err != nil {
    98→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
    99→	}
   100→	fmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatusShort(effectiveSettings))
   101→	fmt.Fprintln(w) // blank line
   102→
   103→	// Show project settings if it exists
   104→	if projectExists {
   105→		projectSettings, err := settings.LoadFromFile(settingsPath)
   106→		if err != nil {
   107→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to load project settings: %w", err)
   108→		}
   109→		fmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatus("Project", projectSettings))
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	// Show local settings if it exists
   113→	if localExists {
   114→		localSettings, err := settings.LoadFromFile(localSettingsPath)
   115→		if err != nil {
   116→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to load local settings: %w", err)
   117→		}
   118→		fmt.Fprintln(w, formatSettingsStatus("Local", localSettings))
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	if effectiveSettings.Enabled {
   122→		writeActiveSessions(w)
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	return nil
   126→}
   127→
   128→// formatSettingsStatusShort formats a short settings status line.
   129→// Output format: "Enabled (manual-commit)" or "Disabled (auto-commit)"
   130→func formatSettingsStatusShort(settings *EntireSettings) string {
   131→	displayName := settings.Strategy
   132→	if dn, ok := strategyInternalToDisplay[settings.Strategy]; ok {
   133→		displayName = dn
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	if settings.Enabled {
   137→		return fmt.Sprintf("Enabled (%s)", displayName)
   138→	}
   139→	return fmt.Sprintf("Disabled (%s)", displayName)
   140→}
   141→
   142→// formatSettingsStatus formats a settings status line with source prefix.
   143→// Output format: "Project, enabled (manual-commit)" or "Local, disabled (auto-commit)"
   144→func formatSettingsStatus(prefix string, settings *EntireSettings) string {
   145→	displayName := settings.Strategy
   146→	if dn, ok := strategyInternalToDisplay[settings.Strategy]; ok {
   147→		displayName = dn
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	if settings.Enabled {
   151→		return fmt.Sprintf("%s, enabled (%s)", prefix, displayName)
   152→	}
   153→	return fmt.Sprintf("%s, disabled (%s)", prefix, displayName)
   154→}
   155→
   156→// timeAgo formats a time as a human-readable relative duration.
   157→func timeAgo(t time.Time) string {
   158→	d := time.Since(t)
   159→	switch {
   160→	case d < time.Minute:
   161→		return "just now"
   162→	case d < time.Hour:
   163→		m := int(d.Minutes())
   164→		return fmt.Sprintf("%dm ago", m)
   165→	case d < 24*time.Hour:
   166→		h := int(d.Hours())
   167→		return fmt.Sprintf("%dh ago", h)
   168→	default:
   169→		days := int(d.Hours() / 24)
   170→		return fmt.Sprintf("%dd ago", days)
   171→	}
   172→}
   173→
   174→// worktreeGroup groups sessions by worktree path for display.
   175→type worktreeGroup struct {
   176→	path     string
   177→	branch   string
   178→	sessions []*session.State
   179→}
   180→
   181→const unknownPlaceholder = "(unknown)"
   182→
   183→// writeActiveSessions writes active session information grouped by worktree.
   184→func writeActiveSessions(w io.Writer) {
   185→	store, err := session.NewStateStore()
   186→	if err != nil {
   187→		return
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	states, err := store.List(context.Background())
   191→	if err != nil || len(states) == 0 {
   192→		return
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Filter to active sessions only
   196→	var active []*session.State
   197→	for _, s := range states {
   198→		if s.EndedAt == nil {
   199→			active = append(active, s)
   200→		}
   201→	}
   202→	if len(active) == 0 {
   203→		return
   204→	}
   205→
   206→	// Group by worktree path
   207→	groups := make(map[string]*worktreeGroup)
   208→	for _, s := range active {
   209→		wp := s.WorktreePath
   210→		if wp == "" {
   211→			wp = unknownPlaceholder
   212→		}
   213→		g, ok := groups[wp]
   214→		if !ok {
   215→			g = &worktreeGroup{path: wp}
   216→			groups[wp] = g
   217→		}
   218→		g.sessions = append(g.sessions, s)
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	// Resolve branch names for each worktree (skip for unknown paths)
   222→	for _, g := range groups {
   223→		if g.path != unknownPlaceholder {
   224→			g.branch = resolveWorktreeBranch(g.path)
   225→		}
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Sort groups: alphabetical by path
   229→	sortedGroups := make([]*worktreeGroup, 0, len(groups))
   230→	for _, g := range groups {
   231→		sortedGroups = append(sortedGroups, g)
   232→	}
   233→	sort.Slice(sortedGroups, func(i, j int) bool {
   234→		return sortedGroups[i].path < sortedGroups[j].path
   235→	})
   236→
   237→	// Sort sessions within each group by StartedAt (newest first)
   238→	for _, g := range sortedGroups {
   239→		sort.Slice(g.sessions, func(i, j int) bool {
   240→			return g.sessions[i].StartedAt.After(g.sessions[j].StartedAt)
   241→		})
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	fmt.Fprintln(w)
   245→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Active Sessions:")
   246→	for i, g := range sortedGroups {
   247→		header := g.path
   248→		if g.branch != "" {
   249→			header += " (" + g.branch + ")"
   250→		}
   251→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s\n", header)
   252→
   253→		for _, st := range g.sessions {
   254→			agentLabel := string(st.AgentType)
   255→			if agentLabel == "" {
   256→				agentLabel = unknownPlaceholder
   257→			}
   258→
   259→			shortID := st.SessionID
   260→			if len(shortID) > 7 {
   261→				shortID = shortID[:7]
   262→			}
   263→
   264→			age := "started " + timeAgo(st.StartedAt)
   265→
   266→			// Show "active X ago" when LastInteractionTime differs meaningfully from StartedAt
   267→			activeStr := ""
   268→			if st.LastInteractionTime != nil && st.LastInteractionTime.Sub(st.StartedAt) > time.Minute {
   269→				activeStr = ", active " + timeAgo(*st.LastInteractionTime)
   270→			}
   271→
   272→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "    [%s] %-9s %s%s\n",
   273→				agentLabel, shortID, age, activeStr)
   274→
   275→			// Show first prompt on indented second line
   276→			if st.FirstPrompt != "" {
   277→				prompt := stringutil.TruncateRunes(st.FirstPrompt, 60, "...")
   278→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "      \"%s\"\n", prompt)
   279→			}
   280→		}
   281→
   282→		// Blank line between groups, but not after the last one
   283→		if i < len(sortedGroups)-1 {
   284→			fmt.Fprintln(w)
   285→		}
   286→	}
   287→}
   288→
   289→// resolveWorktreeBranch resolves the current branch for a worktree path.
   290→func resolveWorktreeBranch(worktreePath string) string {
   291→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(context.Background(), "git", "-C", worktreePath, "rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD")
   292→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   293→	if err != nil {
   294→		return ""
   295→	}
   296→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   297→}
   298→

They answer \"why/what\" (human intent)\n// while checkpoints answer \"how/when\" (machine snapshots).\npackage trail\n\nimport (\n\t\"crypto/rand\"\n\t\"encoding/hex\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"regexp\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\n// TrailID is a 12-character hex identifier for trails.\nconst trailIDLength = 6 // 6 bytes = 12 hex chars\n\n// TrailID is a 12-character hex identifier for trails.\ntype TrailID string\n\n// EmptyTrailID represents an unset or invalid trail ID.\nconst EmptyTrailID TrailID = \"\"\n\n// trailIDRegex validates the format: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.\nvar trailIDRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{12}$`)\n\n// GenerateID creates a new random 12-character hex trail ID.\nfunc GenerateID() (TrailID, error) {\n\tbytes := make([]byte, trailIDLength)\n\tif _, err := rand.Read(bytes); err != nil {\n\t\treturn EmptyTrailID, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to generate random trail ID: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn TrailID(hex.EncodeToString(bytes)), nil\n}\n\n// ValidateID checks if a string is a valid trail ID format.\nfunc ValidateID(s string) error {\n\tif !trailIDRegex.MatchString(s) {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid trail ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters\", s)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// String returns the trail ID as a string.\nfunc (id TrailID) String() string {\n\treturn string(id)\n}\n\n// IsEmpty returns true if the trail ID is empty or unset.\nfunc (id TrailID) IsEmpty() bool {\n\treturn id == EmptyTrailID\n}\n\n// Path returns the sharded storage path for this trail ID.\n// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.\n// Example: \"a3b2c4d5e6f7\" -> \"a3/b2c4d5e6f7\"\nfunc (id TrailID) Path() string {\n\tif len(id) < 3 {\n\t\treturn string(id)\n\t}\n\treturn string(id[:2]) + \"/\" + string(id[2:])\n}\n\n// TrailStatus represents the lifecycle status of a trail.\ntype TrailStatus string\n\nconst (\n\tStatusDraft      TrailStatus = \"draft\"\n\tStatusOpen       TrailStatus = \"open\"\n\tStatusInProgress TrailStatus = \"in_progress\"\n\tStatusInReview   TrailStatus = \"in_review\"\n\tStatusDone       TrailStatus = \"done\"\n\tStatusClosed     TrailStatus = \"closed\"\n)\n\n// ValidStatuses returns all valid trail statuses in lifecycle order.\nfunc ValidStatuses() []TrailStatus {\n\treturn []TrailStatus{\n\t\tStatusDraft,\n\t\tStatusOpen,\n\t\tStatusInProgress,\n\t\tStatusInReview,\n\t\tStatusDone,\n\t\tStatusClosed,\n\t}\n}\n\n// IsValid returns true if the status is a recognized trail status.\nfunc (s TrailStatus) IsValid() bool {\n\tfor _, vs := range ValidStatuses() {\n\t\tif s == vs {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\n// TrailMetadata represents the metadata for a trail, matching the web PR format.\ntype TrailMetadata struct {\n\tTrailID     TrailID     `json:\"trail_id\"`\n\tBranch      string      `json:\"branch\"`\n\tBase        string      `json:\"base\"`\n\tTitle       string      `json:\"title\"`\n\tDescription string      `json:\"description\"`\n\tStatus      TrailStatus `json:\"status\"`\n\tAuthor      string      `json:\"author\"`\n\tAssignees   []string    `json:\"assignees\"`\n\tLabels      []string    `json:\"labels\"`\n\tCreatedAt   time.Time   `json:\"created_at\"`\n\tUpdatedAt   time.Time   `json:\"updated_at\"`\n\tMergedAt    *time.Time  `json:\"merged_at,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// TrailDiscussion holds the discussion/comments for a trail.\ntype TrailDiscussion struct {\n\tComments []TrailComment `json:\"comments\"`\n}\n\n// TrailComment represents a single comment on a trail.\ntype TrailComment struct {\n\tID        string              `json:\"id\"`\n\tAuthor    string              `json:\"author\"`\n\tBody      string              `json:\"body\"`\n\tCreatedAt time.Time           `json:\"created_at\"`\n\tReplies   []TrailCommentReply `json:\"replies,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// TrailCommentReply represents a reply to a comment.\ntype TrailCommentReply struct {\n\tID        string    `json:\"id\"`\n\tAuthor    string    `json:\"author\"`\n\tBody      string    `json:\"body\"`\n\tCreatedAt time.Time `json:\"created_at\"`\n}\n\n// commonBranchPrefixes are stripped from branch names when humanizing.\nvar commonBranchPrefixes = []string{\n\t\"feature/\",\n\t\"fix/\",\n\t\"bugfix/\",\n\t\"chore/\",\n\t\"hotfix/\",\n\t\"release/\",\n}\n\n// HumanizeBranchName converts a branch name into a human-readable title.\n// It strips common prefixes (feature/, fix/, etc.), replaces dashes/underscores\n// with spaces, and capitalizes the first word.\nfunc HumanizeBranchName(branch string) string {\n\tname := branch\n\tfor _, prefix := range commonBranchPrefixes {\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(name, prefix) {\n\t\t\tname = strings.TrimPrefix(name, prefix)\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Replace - and _ with spaces\n\tname = strings.NewReplacer(\"-\", \" \", \"_\", \" \").Replace(name)\n\n\t// Trim spaces and capitalize first letter\n\tname = strings.TrimSpace(name)\n\tif name == \"\" {\n\t\treturn branch\n\t}\n\n\t// Capitalize first character\n\treturn strings.ToUpper(name[:1]) + name[1:]\n}\n"
}

(\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/filemode\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object\"\n)\n\nconst (\n\tmetadataFile   = \"metadata.json\"\n\tdiscussionFile = \"discussion.json\"\n)\n\n// TrailStore provides CRUD operations for trail metadata on the entire/trails branch.\ntype TrailStore struct {\n\trepo *git.Repository\n}\n\n// NewTrailStore creates a new trail store backed by the given git repository.\nfunc NewTrailStore(repo *git.Repository) *TrailStore {\n\treturn &TrailStore{repo: repo}\n}\n\n// EnsureBranch creates the entire/trails orphan branch if it doesn't exist.\nfunc (s *TrailStore) EnsureBranch() error {\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName)\n\t_, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\treturn nil // Branch already exists\n\t}\n\n\t// Create orphan branch with empty tree\n\temptyTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, make(map[string]object.TreeEntry))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to build empty tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)\n\tcommitHash, err := s.createCommit(emptyTreeHash, plumbing.ZeroHash, \"Initialize trails branch\", authorName, authorEmail)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create initial commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tnewRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)\n\tif err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to set branch reference: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Write writes trail metadata and discussion to the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *TrailStore) Write(metadata *TrailMetadata, discussion *TrailDiscussion) error {\n\tif metadata.TrailID.IsEmpty() {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"trail ID is required\")\n\t}\n\n\tif err := s.EnsureBranch(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to ensure trails branch: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get current branch tree\n\tref, entries, err := s.getBranchEntries()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get branch entries: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Build sharded path\n\tbasePath := metadata.TrailID.Path() + \"/\"\n\n\t// Create metadata blob\n\tmetadataJSON, err := json.MarshalIndent(metadata, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal metadata: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tmetadataBlob, err := checkpoint.CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create metadata blob: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tentries[basePath+metadataFile] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\tName: basePath + metadataFile,\n\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\tHash: metadataBlob,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create discussion blob\n\tif discussion == nil {\n\t\tdiscussion = &TrailDiscussion{Comments: []TrailComment{}}\n\t}\n\tdiscussionJSON, err := json.MarshalIndent(discussion, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal discussion: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdiscussionBlob, err := checkpoint.CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, discussionJSON)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create discussion blob: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tentries[basePath+discussionFile] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\tName: basePath + discussionFile,\n\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\tHash: discussionBlob,\n\t}\n\n\t// Build tree and commit\n\tnewTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to build tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)\n\tcommitMsg := fmt.Sprintf(\"Trail: %s (%s)\", metadata.Title, metadata.TrailID)\n\tcommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Update branch ref\n\tnewRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName), commitHash)\n\tif err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to update branch reference: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Read reads a trail by its ID from the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *TrailStore) Read(trailID TrailID) (*TrailMetadata, *TrailDiscussion, error) {\n\ttree, err := s.getBranchTree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tbasePath := trailID.Path() + \"/\"\n\n\t// Read metadata\n\tmetadataEntry, err := tree.FindEntry(basePath + metadataFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"trail %s not found: %w\", trailID, err)\n\t}\n\tmetadataBlob, err := s.repo.BlobObject(metadataEntry.Hash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read metadata blob: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tmetadataReader, err := metadataBlob.Reader()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open metadata reader: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer metadataReader.Close()\n\n\tvar metadata TrailMetadata\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(metadataReader).Decode(&metadata); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to decode metadata: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Read discussion (optional, may not exist yet)\n\tvar discussion TrailDiscussion\n\tdiscussionEntry, err := tree.FindEntry(basePath + discussionFile)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tdiscussionBlob, blobErr := s.repo.BlobObject(discussionEntry.Hash)\n\t\tif blobErr == nil {\n\t\t\tdiscussionReader, readerErr := discussionBlob.Reader()\n\t\t\tif readerErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\t_ = json.NewDecoder(discussionReader).Decode(&discussion)\n\t\t\t\tdiscussionReader.Close()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn &metadata, &discussion, nil\n}\n\n// FindByBranch finds a trail for the given branch name.\n// Returns nil, nil if no trail exists for the branch.\nfunc (s *TrailStore) FindByBranch(branchName string) (*TrailMetadata, error) {\n\ttrails, err := s.List()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, t := range trails {\n\t\tif t.Branch == branchName {\n\t\t\treturn t, nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil, nil\n}\n\n// List returns all trail metadata from the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *TrailStore) List() ([]*TrailMetadata, error) {\n\ttree, err := s.getBranchTree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// Branch doesn't exist yet — no trails\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilerr // Expected when no trails exist yet\n\t}\n\n\tvar trails []*TrailMetadata\n\tentries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)\n\tif err := checkpoint.FlattenTree(s.repo, tree, \"\", entries); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to flatten tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Find all metadata.json files\n\tfor path, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif !strings.HasSuffix(path, \"/\"+metadataFile) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tblob, err := s.repo.BlobObject(entry.Hash)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\treader, err := blob.Reader()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar metadata TrailMetadata\n\t\tdecodeErr := json.NewDecoder(reader).Decode(&metadata)\n\t\treader.Close()\n\t\tif decodeErr != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttrails = append(trails, &metadata)\n\t}\n\n\treturn trails, nil\n}\n\n// Update updates an existing trail's metadata. It reads the current metadata,\n// applies the provided update function, and writes it back.\nfunc (s *TrailStore) Update(trailID TrailID, updateFn func(*TrailMetadata)) error {\n\tmetadata, discussion, err := s.Read(trailID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read trail for update: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tupdateFn(metadata)\n\tmetadata.UpdatedAt = time.Now()\n\n\treturn s.Write(metadata, discussion)\n}\n\n// Delete removes a trail from the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *TrailStore) Delete(trailID TrailID) error {\n\tref, entries, err := s.getBranchEntries()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get branch entries: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tbasePath := trailID.Path() + \"/\"\n\n\t// Remove entries for this trail\n\tfound := false\n\tfor path := range entries {\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(path, basePath) {\n\t\t\tdelete(entries, path)\n\t\t\tfound = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif !found {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"trail %s not found\", trailID)\n\t}\n\n\t// Build tree and commit\n\tnewTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to build tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)\n\tcommitMsg := fmt.Sprintf(\"Delete trail: %s\", trailID)\n\tcommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tnewRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName), commitHash)\n\tif err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to update branch reference: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// getBranchTree returns the tree for the entire/trails branch HEAD.\nfunc (s *TrailStore) getBranchTree() (*object.Tree, error) {\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName)\n\tref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// Try remote tracking branch\n\t\tremoteRefName := plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName(\"origin\", paths.TrailsBranchName)\n\t\tref, err = s.repo.Reference(remoteRefName, true)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"trails branch not found: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tcommit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\ttree, err := commit.Tree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn tree, nil\n}\n\n// getBranchEntries returns the current branch reference and a flat map of all tree entries.\nfunc (s *TrailStore) getBranchEntries() (*plumbing.Reference, map[string]object.TreeEntry, error) {\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName)\n\tref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"trails branch not found: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tcommit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\ttree, err := commit.Tree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tentries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)\n\tif err := checkpoint.FlattenTree(s.repo, tree, \"\", entries); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to flatten tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn ref, entries, nil\n}\n\n// createCommit creates a commit on the trails branch.\nfunc (s *TrailStore) createCommit(treeHash, parentHash plumbing.Hash, message, authorName, authorEmail string) (plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tsig := object.Signature{\n\t\tName:  authorName,\n\t\tEmail: authorEmail,\n\t\tWhen:  now,\n\t}\n\n\tcommit := &object.Commit{\n\t\tTreeHash:  treeHash,\n\t\tAuthor:    sig,\n\t\tCommitter: sig,\n\t\tMessage:   message,\n\t}\n\n\tif parentHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {\n\t\tcommit.ParentHashes = []plumbing.Hash{parentHash}\n\t}\n\n\tobj := s.repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()\n\tif err := commit.Encode(obj); err != nil {\n\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to encode commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\thash, err := s.repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to store commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn hash, nil\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package cli\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"sort\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/trail\"\n\n\t\"github.com/charmbracelet/huh\"\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5\"\n\t\"github.com/spf13/cobra\"\n)\n\nfunc newTrailCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\tcmd := &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"trail\",\n\t\tShort: \"Manage trails for your branches\",\n\t\tLong: `Trails are branch-centric work tracking abstractions. They describe the\n\"why\" and \"what\" of your work, while checkpoints capture the \"how\" and \"when\".\n\nRunning 'entire trail' without a subcommand shows the trail for the current\nbranch, or lists all trails if no trail exists for the current branch.`,\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\treturn runTrailShow(cmd.OutOrStdout())\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tcmd.AddCommand(newTrailListCmd())\n\tcmd.AddCommand(newTrailCreateCmd())\n\tcmd.AddCommand(newTrailUpdateCmd())\n\n\treturn cmd\n}\n\n// runTrailShow shows the trail for the current branch, or falls through to list.\nfunc runTrailShow(w io.Writer) error {\n\tbranch, err := GetCurrentBranch()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn runTrailListAll(w, \"\", false)\n\t}\n\n\trepo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tstore := trail.NewTrailStore(repo)\n\tmetadata, err := store.FindByBranch(branch)\n\tif err != nil || metadata == nil {\n\t\treturn runTrailListAll(w, \"\", false)\n\t}\n\n\tprintTrailDetails(w, metadata)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc printTrailDetails(w io.Writer, m *trail.TrailMetadata) {\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"Trail: %s\\n\", m.Title)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  ID:      %s\\n\", m.TrailID)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  Branch:  %s\\n\", m.Branch)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  Base:    %s\\n\", m.Base)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  Status:  %s\\n\", m.Status)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  Author:  %s\\n\", m.Author)\n\tif m.Description != \"\" {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  Description: %s\\n\", m.Description)\n\t}\n\tif len(m.Labels) > 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  Labels:  %s\\n\", strings.Join(m.Labels, \", \"))\n\t}\n\tif len(m.Assignees) > 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  Assignees: %s\\n\", strings.Join(m.Assignees, \", \"))\n\t}\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  Created: %s\\n\", m.CreatedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"  Updated: %s\\n\", m.UpdatedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))\n}\n\nfunc newTrailListCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\tvar statusFilter string\n\tvar jsonOutput bool\n\n\tcmd := &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"list\",\n\t\tShort: \"List all trails\",\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\treturn runTrailListAll(cmd.OutOrStdout(), statusFilter, jsonOutput)\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&statusFilter, \"status\", \"\", \"Filter by status (draft, open, in_progress, in_review, done, closed)\")\n\tcmd.Flags().BoolVar(&jsonOutput, \"json\", false, \"Output as JSON\")\n\n\treturn cmd\n}\n\nfunc runTrailListAll(w io.Writer, statusFilter string, jsonOutput bool) error {\n\trepo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tstore := trail.NewTrailStore(repo)\n\ttrails, err := store.List()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to list trails: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif trails == nil {\n\t\ttrails = []*trail.TrailMetadata{}\n\t}\n\n\t// Apply status filter\n\tif statusFilter != \"\" {\n\t\tstatus := trail.TrailStatus(statusFilter)\n\t\tif !status.IsValid() {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid status %q: valid values are %s\", statusFilter, formatValidStatuses())\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar filtered []*trail.TrailMetadata\n\t\tfor _, t := range trails {\n\t\t\tif t.Status == status {\n\t\t\t\tfiltered = append(filtered, t)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\ttrails = filtered\n\t}\n\n\t// Sort by updated_at descending\n\tsort.Slice(trails, func(i, j int) bool {\n\t\treturn trails[i].UpdatedAt.After(trails[j].UpdatedAt)\n\t})\n\n\tif jsonOutput {\n\t\tenc := json.NewEncoder(w)\n\t\tenc.SetIndent(\"\", \"  \")\n\t\treturn enc.Encode(trails)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(trails) == 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"No trails found.\")\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(w, \"Create one with: entire trail create\")\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Table output\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"%-30s %-40s %-13s %-15s %s\\n\", \"BRANCH\", \"TITLE\", \"STATUS\", \"AUTHOR\", \"UPDATED\")\n\tfor _, t := range trails {\n\t\tbranch := truncate(t.Branch, 30)\n\t\ttitle := truncate(t.Title, 40)\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"%-30s %-40s %-13s %-15s %s\\n\",\n\t\t\tbranch, title, t.Status, truncate(t.Author, 15), timeAgo(t.UpdatedAt))\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc newTrailCreateCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\tvar title, description, base, branch, status string\n\n\tcmd := &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"create\",\n\t\tShort: \"Create a trail for the current or a new branch\",\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\treturn runTrailCreate(cmd.OutOrStdout(), cmd.ErrOrStderr(), title, description, base, branch, status)\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&title, \"title\", \"\", \"Trail title\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&description, \"description\", \"\", \"Trail description\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&base, \"base\", \"\", \"Base branch (defaults to detected default branch)\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&branch, \"branch\", \"\", \"Branch for the trail (defaults to current branch)\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&status, \"status\", \"\", \"Initial status (defaults to draft)\")\n\n\treturn cmd\n}\n\nfunc runTrailCreate(w, errW io.Writer, title, description, base, branch, statusStr string) error {\n\trepo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Determine branch\n\tif branch == \"\" {\n\t\tbranch, err = GetCurrentBranch()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to determine current branch: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if on default branch\n\tisDefault, _, _ := IsOnDefaultBranch()\n\tif isDefault && branch == \"\" {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(errW, \"Cannot create a trail on the default branch.\")\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(errW, \"Create or switch to a feature branch first.\")\n\t\treturn NewSilentError(fmt.Errorf(\"cannot create trail on default branch\"))\n\t}\n\n\t// Determine base branch\n\tif base == \"\" {\n\t\tbase = strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo)\n\t\tif base == \"\" {\n\t\t\tbase = \"main\"\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if trail already exists for this branch\n\tstore := trail.NewTrailStore(repo)\n\texisting, err := store.FindByBranch(branch)\n\tif err == nil && existing != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"Trail already exists for branch %q (ID: %s)\\n\", branch, existing.TrailID)\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Determine title\n\tif title == \"\" {\n\t\tdefaultTitle := trail.HumanizeBranchName(branch)\n\n\t\t// Interactive mode if flags not provided\n\t\tif !hasFlag(\"title\") {\n\t\t\tvar inputTitle string\n\t\t\tform := NewAccessibleForm(\n\t\t\t\thuh.NewGroup(\n\t\t\t\t\thuh.NewInput().\n\t\t\t\t\t\tTitle(\"Trail title\").\n\t\t\t\t\t\tPlaceholder(defaultTitle).\n\t\t\t\t\t\tValue(&inputTitle),\n\t\t\t\t),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t\tif formErr := form.Run(); formErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"form cancelled: %w\", formErr)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif inputTitle != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\ttitle = inputTitle\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\ttitle = defaultTitle\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\ttitle = defaultTitle\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Determine status\n\tvar trailStatus trail.TrailStatus\n\tif statusStr != \"\" {\n\t\ttrailStatus = trail.TrailStatus(statusStr)\n\t\tif !trailStatus.IsValid() {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid status %q: valid values are %s\", statusStr, formatValidStatuses())\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\ttrailStatus = trail.StatusDraft\n\t}\n\n\t// Generate trail ID\n\ttrailID, err := trail.GenerateID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to generate trail ID: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get git author\n\tauthorName, _ := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)\n\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tmetadata := &trail.TrailMetadata{\n\t\tTrailID:     trailID,\n\t\tBranch:      branch,\n\t\tBase:        base,\n\t\tTitle:       title,\n\t\tDescription: description,\n\t\tStatus:      trailStatus,\n\t\tAuthor:      authorName,\n\t\tAssignees:   []string{},\n\t\tLabels:      []string{},\n\t\tCreatedAt:   now,\n\t\tUpdatedAt:   now,\n\t}\n\n\tif err := store.Write(metadata, nil); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"Created trail %q for branch %s (ID: %s)\\n\", title, branch, trailID)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc newTrailUpdateCmd() *cobra.Command {\n\tvar statusStr, title, description, branch string\n\tvar labelAdd, labelRemove []string\n\n\tcmd := &cobra.Command{\n\t\tUse:   \"update\",\n\t\tShort: \"Update trail metadata\",\n\t\tRunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {\n\t\t\treturn runTrailUpdate(cmd.OutOrStdout(), statusStr, title, description, branch, labelAdd, labelRemove)\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&statusStr, \"status\", \"\", \"Update status\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&title, \"title\", \"\", \"Update title\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&description, \"description\", \"\", \"Update description\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringVar(&branch, \"branch\", \"\", \"Branch to update trail for (defaults to current)\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringSliceVar(&labelAdd, \"add-label\", nil, \"Add label(s)\")\n\tcmd.Flags().StringSliceVar(&labelRemove, \"remove-label\", nil, \"Remove label(s)\")\n\n\treturn cmd\n}\n\nfunc runTrailUpdate(w io.Writer, statusStr, title, description, branch string, labelAdd, labelRemove []string) error {\n\trepo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open repository: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Determine branch\n\tif branch == \"\" {\n\t\tbranch, err = GetCurrentBranch()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to determine current branch: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tstore := trail.NewTrailStore(repo)\n\tmetadata, err := store.FindByBranch(branch)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to find trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif metadata == nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"no trail found for branch %q\", branch)\n\t}\n\n\t// Validate status if provided\n\tif statusStr != \"\" {\n\t\tstatus := trail.TrailStatus(statusStr)\n\t\tif !status.IsValid() {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid status %q: valid values are %s\", statusStr, formatValidStatuses())\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\terr = store.Update(metadata.TrailID, func(m *trail.TrailMetadata) {\n\t\tif statusStr != \"\" {\n\t\t\tm.Status = trail.TrailStatus(statusStr)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif title != \"\" {\n\t\t\tm.Title = title\n\t\t}\n\t\tif description != \"\" {\n\t\t\tm.Description = description\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, l := range labelAdd {\n\t\t\tif !containsString(m.Labels, l) {\n\t\t\t\tm.Labels = append(m.Labels, l)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, l := range labelRemove {\n\t\t\tm.Labels = removeString(m.Labels, l)\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to update trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(w, \"Updated trail for branch %s\\n\", branch)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// AutoCreateTrail creates a trail automatically for the current branch if one doesn't exist.\n// This is called during session start for non-main branches.\nfunc AutoCreateTrail(repo *git.Repository, branchName, baseBranch string) error {\n\tstore := trail.NewTrailStore(repo)\n\n\texisting, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)\n\tif err == nil && existing != nil {\n\t\treturn nil // Trail already exists\n\t}\n\n\ttrailID, err := trail.GenerateID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to generate trail ID: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, _ := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\n\tmetadata := &trail.TrailMetadata{\n\t\tTrailID:     trailID,\n\t\tBranch:      branchName,\n\t\tBase:        baseBranch,\n\t\tTitle:       trail.HumanizeBranchName(branchName),\n\t\tStatus:      trail.StatusInProgress,\n\t\tAuthor:      authorName,\n\t\tAssignees:   []string{},\n\t\tLabels:      []string{},\n\t\tCreatedAt:   now,\n\t\tUpdatedAt:   now,\n\t}\n\n\tif err := store.Write(metadata, nil); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create trail: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] Created trail for branch: %s\\n\", branchName)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// hasFlag is a simple helper that checks os.Args for --flag presence.\n// Used to distinguish between \"flag not provided\" and \"flag provided with empty value\".\nfunc hasFlag(name string) bool {\n\tfor _, arg := range os.Args {\n\t\tif arg == \"--\"+name || strings.HasPrefix(arg, \"--\"+name+\"=\") {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc truncate(s string, maxLen int) string {\n\tif len(s) <= maxLen {\n\t\treturn s\n\t}\n\tif maxLen <= 3 {\n\t\treturn s[:maxLen]\n\t}\n\treturn s[:maxLen-3] + \"...\"\n}\n\nfunc formatValidStatuses() string {\n\tstatuses := trail.ValidStatuses()\n\tnames := make([]string, len(statuses))\n\tfor i, s := range statuses {\n\t\tnames[i] = string(s)\n\t}\n\treturn strings.Join(names, \", \")\n}\n\nfunc containsString(slice []string, s string) bool {\n\tfor _, v := range slice {\n\t\tif v == s {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc removeString(slice []string, s string) []string {\n\tvar result []string\n\tfor _, v := range slice {\n\t\tif v != s {\n\t\t\tresult = append(result, v)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn result\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/trail/trail_test.go",
  "content": "package trail\n\nimport (\n\t\"testing\"\n)\n\nfunc TestGenerateID(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tid, err := GenerateID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GenerateID() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(id) != 12 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected 12-char ID, got %d: %q\", len(id), id)\n\t}\n\tif err := ValidateID(id.String()); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"generated ID failed validation: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestGenerateID_Unique(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tseen := make(map[TrailID]bool)\n\tfor i := 0; i < 100; i++ {\n\t\tid, err := GenerateID()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GenerateID() error = %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif seen[id] {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"duplicate ID generated: %s\", id)\n\t\t}\n\t\tseen[id] = true\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestValidateID(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname    string\n\t\tid      string\n\t\twantErr bool\n\t}{\n\t\t{\"valid\", \"abcdef123456\", false},\n\t\t{\"valid_all_hex\", \"0123456789ab\", false},\n\t\t{\"too_short\", \"abcdef\", true},\n\t\t{\"too_long\", \"abcdef1234567\", true},\n\t\t{\"uppercase\", \"ABCDEF123456\", true},\n\t\t{\"non_hex\", \"ghijkl123456\", true},\n\t\t{\"empty\", \"\", true},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tt.Parallel()\n\t\t\terr := ValidateID(tt.id)\n\t\t\tif (err != nil) != tt.wantErr {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"ValidateID(%q) error = %v, wantErr %v\", tt.id, err, tt.wantErr)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrailID_Path(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tid   TrailID\n\t\twant string\n\t}{\n\t\t{\"abcdef123456\", \"ab/cdef123456\"},\n\t\t{\"0123456789ab\", \"01/23456789ab\"},\n\t\t{\"ab\", \"ab\"},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(string(tt.id), func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tt.Parallel()\n\t\t\tif got := tt.id.Path(); got != tt.want {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"TrailID(%q).Path() = %q, want %q\", tt.id, got, tt.want)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrailID_IsEmpty(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tif !EmptyTrailID.IsEmpty() {\n\t\tt.Error(\"EmptyTrailID.IsEmpty() should return true\")\n\t}\n\tid := TrailID(\"abcdef123456\")\n\tif id.IsEmpty() {\n\t\tt.Error(\"non-empty ID.IsEmpty() should return false\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrailStatus_IsValid(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tstatus TrailStatus\n\t\tvalid  bool\n\t}{\n\t\t{StatusDraft, true},\n\t\t{StatusOpen, true},\n\t\t{StatusInProgress, true},\n\t\t{StatusInReview, true},\n\t\t{StatusDone, true},\n\t\t{StatusClosed, true},\n\t\t{\"invalid\", false},\n\t\t{\"\", false},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(string(tt.status), func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tt.Parallel()\n\t\t\tif got := tt.status.IsValid(); got != tt.valid {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"TrailStatus(%q).IsValid() = %v, want %v\", tt.status, got, tt.valid)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestValidStatuses(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tstatuses := ValidStatuses()\n\tif len(statuses) != 6 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected 6 statuses, got %d\", len(statuses))\n\t}\n\t// Verify lifecycle order\n\texpected := []TrailStatus{StatusDraft, StatusOpen, StatusInProgress, StatusInReview, StatusDone, StatusClosed}\n\tfor i, s := range expected {\n\t\tif statuses[i] != s {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"status[%d] = %q, want %q\", i, statuses[i], s)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestHumanizeBranchName(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\ttests := []struct {\n\t\tname   string\n\t\tbranch string\n\t\twant   string\n\t}{\n\t\t{\"feature prefix\", \"feature/add-auth\", \"Add auth\"},\n\t\t{\"fix prefix\", \"fix/login-bug\", \"Login bug\"},\n\t\t{\"bugfix prefix\", \"bugfix/typo-fix\", \"Typo fix\"},\n\t\t{\"chore prefix\", \"chore/update-deps\", \"Update deps\"},\n\t\t{\"hotfix prefix\", \"hotfix/security-patch\", \"Security patch\"},\n\t\t{\"release prefix\", \"release/v2.0\", \"V2.0\"},\n\t\t{\"no prefix\", \"add-auth\", \"Add auth\"},\n\t\t{\"underscores\", \"add_user_auth\", \"Add user auth\"},\n\t\t{\"mixed separators\", \"fix/some_complex-name\", \"Some complex name\"},\n\t\t{\"simple name\", \"main\", \"Main\"},\n\t\t{\"empty after prefix\", \"feature/\", \"feature/\"},\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, tt := range tests {\n\t\tt.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tt.Parallel()\n\t\t\tif got := HumanizeBranchName(tt.branch); got != tt.want {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"HumanizeBranchName(%q) = %q, want %q\", tt.branch, got, tt.want)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "package trail\n\nimport (\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5\"\n)\n\n// initTestRepo creates a test git repository with an initial commit.\nfunc initTestRepo(t *testing.T) *git.Repository {\n\tt.Helper()\n\n\tdir := t.TempDir()\n\n\t// Use git CLI to init and create initial commit (go-git needs at least one commit)\n\tcmds := [][]string{\n\t\t{\"git\", \"init\", dir},\n\t\t{\"git\", \"-C\", dir, \"config\", \"user.name\", \"Test\"},\n\t\t{\"git\", \"-C\", dir, \"config\", \"user.email\", \"test@test.com\"},\n\t}\n\tfor _, args := range cmds {\n\t\tcmd := exec.Command(args[0], args[1:]...)\n\t\tif out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"command %v failed: %v\\n%s\", args, err, out)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a file and commit\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, \"README.md\"), []byte(\"# Test\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to write file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tcmds = [][]string{\n\t\t{\"git\", \"-C\", dir, \"add\", \".\"},\n\t\t{\"git\", \"-C\", dir, \"commit\", \"-m\", \"Initial commit\"},\n\t}\n\tfor _, args := range cmds {\n\t\tcmd := exec.Command(args[0], args[1:]...)\n\t\tif out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"command %v failed: %v\\n%s\", args, err, out)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to open repo: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn repo\n}\n\nfunc TestTrailStore_EnsureBranch(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewTrailStore(repo)\n\n\t// First call should create the branch\n\tif err := store.EnsureBranch(); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"EnsureBranch() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Second call should be idempotent\n\tif err := store.EnsureBranch(); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"EnsureBranch() second call error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrailStore_WriteAndRead(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewTrailStore(repo)\n\n\ttrailID, err := GenerateID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GenerateID() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tnow := time.Now().Truncate(time.Second)\n\tmetadata := &TrailMetadata{\n\t\tTrailID:     trailID,\n\t\tBranch:      \"feature/test\",\n\t\tBase:        \"main\",\n\t\tTitle:       \"Test trail\",\n\t\tDescription: \"A test trail\",\n\t\tStatus:      StatusDraft,\n\t\tAuthor:      \"tester\",\n\t\tAssignees:   []string{},\n\t\tLabels:      []string{\"test\"},\n\t\tCreatedAt:   now,\n\t\tUpdatedAt:   now,\n\t}\n\n\tdiscussion := &TrailDiscussion{Comments: []TrailComment{}}\n\n\tif err := store.Write(metadata, discussion); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Write() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Read it back\n\tgotMeta, gotDisc, err := store.Read(trailID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Read() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif gotMeta.TrailID != trailID {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() trail_id = %s, want %s\", gotMeta.TrailID, trailID)\n\t}\n\tif gotMeta.Branch != \"feature/test\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() branch = %q, want %q\", gotMeta.Branch, \"feature/test\")\n\t}\n\tif gotMeta.Title != \"Test trail\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() title = %q, want %q\", gotMeta.Title, \"Test trail\")\n\t}\n\tif gotMeta.Status != StatusDraft {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() status = %q, want %q\", gotMeta.Status, StatusDraft)\n\t}\n\tif len(gotMeta.Labels) != 1 || gotMeta.Labels[0] != \"test\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() labels = %v, want [test]\", gotMeta.Labels)\n\t}\n\tif gotDisc == nil {\n\t\tt.Error(\"Read() discussion should not be nil\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrailStore_FindByBranch(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewTrailStore(repo)\n\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\n\t// Create two trails for different branches\n\tfor _, branch := range []string{\"feature/a\", \"feature/b\"} {\n\t\tid, _ := GenerateID()\n\t\tmeta := &TrailMetadata{\n\t\t\tTrailID:   id,\n\t\t\tBranch:    branch,\n\t\t\tBase:      \"main\",\n\t\t\tTitle:     HumanizeBranchName(branch),\n\t\t\tStatus:    StatusDraft,\n\t\t\tAuthor:    \"test\",\n\t\t\tAssignees: []string{},\n\t\t\tLabels:    []string{},\n\t\t\tCreatedAt: now,\n\t\t\tUpdatedAt: now,\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := store.Write(meta, nil); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Write() error = %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Find by branch\n\tfound, err := store.FindByBranch(\"feature/a\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"FindByBranch() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif found == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"FindByBranch() returned nil, expected trail\")\n\t}\n\tif found.Branch != \"feature/a\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"FindByBranch() branch = %q, want %q\", found.Branch, \"feature/a\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Not found\n\tnotFound, err := store.FindByBranch(\"feature/c\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"FindByBranch() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif notFound != nil {\n\t\tt.Error(\"FindByBranch() should return nil for non-existent branch\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrailStore_List(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewTrailStore(repo)\n\n\t// List when no trails exist (branch doesn't exist yet)\n\ttrails, err := store.List()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"List() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif trails != nil {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"List() = %v, want nil for empty store\", trails)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a trail\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tid, _ := GenerateID()\n\tmeta := &TrailMetadata{\n\t\tTrailID:   id,\n\t\tBranch:    \"feature/test\",\n\t\tBase:      \"main\",\n\t\tTitle:     \"Test\",\n\t\tStatus:    StatusDraft,\n\t\tAuthor:    \"test\",\n\t\tAssignees: []string{},\n\t\tLabels:    []string{},\n\t\tCreatedAt: now,\n\t\tUpdatedAt: now,\n\t}\n\tif err := store.Write(meta, nil); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Write() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\ttrails, err = store.List()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"List() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(trails) != 1 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"List() returned %d trails, want 1\", len(trails))\n\t}\n\tif trails[0].TrailID != id {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"List()[0].TrailID = %s, want %s\", trails[0].TrailID, id)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrailStore_Update(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewTrailStore(repo)\n\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tid, _ := GenerateID()\n\tmeta := &TrailMetadata{\n\t\tTrailID:   id,\n\t\tBranch:    \"feature/test\",\n\t\tBase:      \"main\",\n\t\tTitle:     \"Original\",\n\t\tStatus:    StatusDraft,\n\t\tAuthor:    \"test\",\n\t\tAssignees: []string{},\n\t\tLabels:    []string{},\n\t\tCreatedAt: now,\n\t\tUpdatedAt: now,\n\t}\n\tif err := store.Write(meta, nil); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Write() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Update\n\tif err := store.Update(id, func(m *TrailMetadata) {\n\t\tm.Title = \"Updated\"\n\t\tm.Status = StatusInProgress\n\t\tm.Labels = []string{\"urgent\"}\n\t}); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Update() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify\n\tupdated, _, err := store.Read(id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Read() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif updated.Title != \"Updated\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() title = %q, want %q\", updated.Title, \"Updated\")\n\t}\n\tif updated.Status != StatusInProgress {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() status = %q, want %q\", updated.Status, StatusInProgress)\n\t}\n\tif len(updated.Labels) != 1 || updated.Labels[0] != \"urgent\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() labels = %v, want [urgent]\", updated.Labels)\n\t}\n\tif !updated.UpdatedAt.After(now) {\n\t\tt.Error(\"Read() updated_at should be after original\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrailStore_Delete(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewTrailStore(repo)\n\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tid, _ := GenerateID()\n\tmeta := &TrailMetadata{\n\t\tTrailID:   id,\n\t\tBranch:    \"feature/test\",\n\t\tBase:      \"main\",\n\t\tTitle:     \"To delete\",\n\t\tStatus:    StatusDraft,\n\t\tAuthor:    \"test\",\n\t\tAssignees: []string{},\n\t\tLabels:    []string{},\n\t\tCreatedAt: now,\n\t\tUpdatedAt: now,\n\t}\n\tif err := store.Write(meta, nil); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Write() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Delete\n\tif err := store.Delete(id); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Delete() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify it's gone\n\t_, _, err := store.Read(id)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tt.Error(\"Read() should fail after delete\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestTrailStore_ReadNonExistent(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewTrailStore(repo)\n\n\tif err := store.EnsureBranch(); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"EnsureBranch() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t_, _, err := store.Read(TrailID(\"abcdef123456\"))\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tt.Error(\"Read() should fail for non-existent trail\")\n\t}\n}\n"
}

     1→// Package trail provides types and helpers for managing trail metadata.
     2→// Trails are branch-centric work tracking abstractions stored on the
     3→// entire/trails orphan branch. They answer "why/what" (human intent)
     4→// while checkpoints answer "how/when" (machine snapshots).
     5→package trail
     6→
     7→import (
     8→	"crypto/rand"
     9→	"encoding/hex"
    10→	"fmt"
    11→	"regexp"
    12→	"strings"
    13→	"time"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// TrailID is a 12-character hex identifier for trails.
    17→const trailIDLength = 6 // 6 bytes = 12 hex chars
    18→
    19→// TrailID is a 12-character hex identifier for trails.
    20→type TrailID string
    21→
    22→// EmptyTrailID represents an unset or invalid trail ID.
    23→const EmptyTrailID TrailID = ""
    24→
    25→// trailIDRegex validates the format: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
    26→var trailIDRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{12}$`)
    27→
    28→// GenerateID creates a new random 12-character hex trail ID.
    29→func GenerateID() (TrailID, error) {
    30→	bytes := make([]byte, trailIDLength)
    31→	if _, err := rand.Read(bytes); err != nil {
    32→		return EmptyTrailID, fmt.Errorf("failed to generate random trail ID: %w", err)
    33→	}
    34→	return TrailID(hex.EncodeToString(bytes)), nil
    35→}
    36→
    37→// ValidateID checks if a string is a valid trail ID format.
    38→func ValidateID(s string) error {
    39→	if !trailIDRegex.MatchString(s) {
    40→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid trail ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters", s)
    41→	}
    42→	return nil
    43→}
    44→
    45→// String returns the trail ID as a string.
    46→func (id TrailID) String() string {
    47→	return string(id)
    48→}
    49→
    50→// IsEmpty returns true if the trail ID is empty or unset.
    51→func (id TrailID) IsEmpty() bool {
    52→	return id == EmptyTrailID
    53→}
    54→
    55→// Path returns the sharded storage path for this trail ID.
    56→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
    57→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
    58→func (id TrailID) Path() string {
    59→	if len(id) < 3 {
    60→		return string(id)
    61→	}
    62→	return string(id[:2]) + "/" + string(id[2:])
    63→}
    64→
    65→// TrailStatus represents the lifecycle status of a trail.
    66→type TrailStatus string
    67→
    68→const (
    69→	StatusDraft      TrailStatus = "draft"
    70→	StatusOpen       TrailStatus = "open"
    71→	StatusInProgress TrailStatus = "in_progress"
    72→	StatusInReview   TrailStatus = "in_review"
    73→	StatusDone       TrailStatus = "done"
    74→	StatusClosed     TrailStatus = "closed"
    75→)
    76→
    77→// ValidStatuses returns all valid trail statuses in lifecycle order.
    78→func ValidStatuses() []TrailStatus {
    79→	return []TrailStatus{
    80→		StatusDraft,
    81→		StatusOpen,
    82→		StatusInProgress,
    83→		StatusInReview,
    84→		StatusDone,
    85→		StatusClosed,
    86→	}
    87→}
    88→
    89→// IsValid returns true if the status is a recognized trail status.
    90→func (s TrailStatus) IsValid() bool {
    91→	for _, vs := range ValidStatuses() {
    92→		if s == vs {
    93→			return true
    94→		}
    95→	}
    96→	return false
    97→}
    98→
    99→// TrailMetadata represents the metadata for a trail, matching the web PR format.
   100→type TrailMetadata struct {
   101→	TrailID     TrailID     `json:"trail_id"`
   102→	Branch      string      `json:"branch"`
   103→	Base        string      `json:"base"`
   104→	Title       string      `json:"title"`
   105→	Description string      `json:"description"`
   106→	Status      TrailStatus `json:"status"`
   107→	Author      string      `json:"author"`
   108→	Assignees   []string    `json:"assignees"`
   109→	Labels      []string    `json:"labels"`
   110→	CreatedAt   time.Time   `json:"created_at"`
   111→	UpdatedAt   time.Time   `json:"updated_at"`
   112→	MergedAt    *time.Time  `json:"merged_at,omitempty"`
   113→}
   114→
   115→// TrailDiscussion holds the discussion/comments for a trail.
   116→type TrailDiscussion struct {
   117→	Comments []TrailComment `json:"comments"`
   118→}
   119→
   120→// TrailComment represents a single comment on a trail.
   121→type TrailComment struct {
   122→	ID        string              `json:"id"`
   123→	Author    string              `json:"author"`
   124→	Body      string              `json:"body"`
   125→	CreatedAt time.Time           `json:"created_at"`
   126→	Replies   []TrailCommentReply `json:"replies,omitempty"`
   127→}
   128→
   129→// TrailCommentReply represents a reply to a comment.
   130→type TrailCommentReply struct {
   131→	ID        string    `json:"id"`
   132→	Author    string    `json:"author"`
   133→	Body      string    `json:"body"`
   134→	CreatedAt time.Time `json:"created_at"`
   135→}
   136→
   137→// commonBranchPrefixes are stripped from branch names when humanizing.
   138→var commonBranchPrefixes = []string{
   139→	"feature/",
   140→	"fix/",
   141→	"bugfix/",
   142→	"chore/",
   143→	"hotfix/",
   144→	"release/",
   145→}
   146→
   147→// HumanizeBranchName converts a branch name into a human-readable title.
   148→// It strips common prefixes (feature/, fix/, etc.), replaces dashes/underscores
   149→// with spaces, and capitalizes the first word.
   150→func HumanizeBranchName(branch string) string {
   151→	name := branch
   152→	for _, prefix := range commonBranchPrefixes {
   153→		if strings.HasPrefix(name, prefix) {
   154→			name = strings.TrimPrefix(name, prefix)
   155→			break
   156→		}
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	// Replace - and _ with spaces
   160→	name = strings.NewReplacer("-", " ", "_", " ").Replace(name)
   161→
   162→	// Trim spaces and capitalize first letter
   163→	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
   164→	if name == "" {
   165→		return branch
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	// Capitalize first character
   169→	return strings.ToUpper(name[:1]) + name[1:]
   170→}
   171→

CommentReply struct {\n\tID        string    `json:\"id\"`\n\tAuthor    string    `json:\"author\"`\n\tBody      string    `json:\"body\"`\n\tCreatedAt time.Time `json:\"created_at\"`\n}\n\n// commonBranchPrefixes are stripped from branch names when humanizing.\nvar commonBranchPrefixes = []string{\n\t\"feature/\",\n\t\"fix/\",\n\t\"bugfix/\",\n\t\"chore/\",\n\t\"hotfix/\",\n\t\"release/\",\n}\n\n// HumanizeBranchName converts a branch name into a human-readable title.\n// It strips common prefixes (feature/, fix/, etc.), replaces dashes/underscores\n// with spaces, and capitalizes the first word.\nfunc HumanizeBranchName(branch string) string {\n\tname := branch\n\tfor _, prefix := range commonBranchPrefixes {\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(name, prefix) {\n\t\t\tname = strings.TrimPrefix(name, prefix)\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Replace - and _ with spaces\n\tname = strings.NewReplacer(\"-\", \" \", \"_\", \" \").Replace(name)\n\n\t// Trim spaces and capitalize first letter\n\tname = strings.TrimSpace(name)\n\tif name == \"\" {\n\t\treturn branch\n\t}\n\n\t// Capitalize first character\n\treturn strings.ToUpper(name[:1]) + name[1:]\n}\n"
}

(\n\tmetadataFile   = \"metadata.json\"\n\tdiscussionFile = \"discussion.json\"\n)\n\n// ErrTrailNotFound is returned when a trail cannot be found.\nvar ErrTrailNotFound = errors.New(\"trail not given git repository.\nfunc NewStore(repo *git.Repository) *Store {\n\treturn &Store{repo: repo}\n}\n\n// EnsureBranch creates the entire/trails orphan branch if it doesn't exist.\nfunc (s *Store) EnsureBranch() error {\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName)\n\t_, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\treturn nil // Branch already fmt.Errorf(\"failed to build empty tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)\n\tcommitHash, err := s.createCommit(emptyTreeHash, plumbing.ZeroHash,
