and discussion to the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *Store) Write(metadata *Metadata, discussion *Discussion) error {\n\tif metadata.TrailID.IsEmpty() {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"trail ID is required\")\n\t}\n\n\tif err := s.EnsureBranch(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to ensure trails branch: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get current branch tree\n\tref, entries, err := &Discussion{Comments: []Comment{}}\n\t}\n\tdiscussionJSON, err := json.MarshalIndent(discussion, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal discussion: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdiscussionBlob, err := checkpoint.CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, discussionJSON)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create discussion blob: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tentries[basePath+discussionFile] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\tName: basePath + discussionFile,\n\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\tHash: discussionBlob,\n\t}\n\n\t// Build tree and commit\n\tnewTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to build tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)\n\tcommitMsg := fmt.Sprintf(\"Trail: %s (%s)\", metadata.Title, metadata.TrailID)\n\tcommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Update branch ref\n\tnewRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName), commitHash)\n\tif err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to update branch reference: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// Read reads a trail by its ID from the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *Store) Read(trailID ID) (*Metadata, *Discussion, error) {\n\ttree, err := s.getBranchTree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tbasePath := trailID.Path() + \"/\"\n\n\t// Read metadata\n\tmetadataEntry, err := tree.FindEntry(basePath + metadataFile)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"trail %s not found: %w\", trailID, err)\n\t}\n\tmetadataBlob, err := s.repo.BlobObject(metadataEntry.Hash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read metadata blob: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tmetadataReader, err := metadataBlob.Reader()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open metadata reader: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer metadataReader.Close()\n\n\tvar metadata Metadata\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(metadataReader).Decode(&metadata); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to decode metadata: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Read discussion (optional, may not exist yet)\n\tvar discussion Discussion\n\tdiscussionEntry, err := tree.FindEntry(basePath + discussionFile)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tdiscussionBlob, blobErr := s.repo.BlobObject(discussionEntry.Hash)\n\t\tif blobErr == nil {\n\t\t\tdiscussionReader, readerErr := discussionBlob.Reader()\n\t\t\tif readerErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\t//nolint:errcheck // best-effort decode of optional discussion\n\t\t\t\tjson.NewDecoder(discussionReader).Decode(&discussion)\n\t\t\t\t_ = discussionReader.Close()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn &metadata, &discussion, nil\n}\n\n// FindByBranch finds a trail for the given branch name.\n// Returns nil, nil, nil if no trail exists for the branch.\nfunc (s *Store) FindByBranch(branchName string) (*Metadata, error) {\n\ttrails, err := s.List()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, t := range trails {\n\t\tif t.Branch == branchName {\n\t\t\treturn t, nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil, nil means \"not found\" — callers check both\n}\n\n// List returns all trail metadata from the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *Store) List() ([]*Metadata, error) {\n\ttree, err := s.getBranchTree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// Branch doesn't exist yet — no trails\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilerr // Expected when no trails exist yet\n\t}\n\n\tvar trails []*Metadata\n\tentries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)\n\tif err := checkpoint.FlattenTree(s.repo, tree, \"\", entries); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to flatten tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Find all metadata.json files\n\tfor path, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif !strings.HasSuffix(path, \"/\"+metadataFile) {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tblob, err := s.repo.BlobObject(entry.Hash)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\treader, err := blob.Reader()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar metadata Metadata\n\t\tdecodeErr := json.NewDecoder(reader).Decode(&metadata)\n\t\t_ = reader.Close()\n\t\tif decodeErr != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttrails = append(trails, &metadata)\n\t}\n\n\treturn trails, nil\n}\n\n// Update updates an existing trail's metadata. It reads the current metadata,\n// applies the provided update function, and writes it back.\nfunc (s *Store) Update(trailID ID, updateFn func(*Metadata)) error {\n\tmetadata, discussion, err := s.Read(trailID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read trail for update: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tupdateFn(metadata)\n\tmetadata.UpdatedAt = time.Now()\n\n\treturn s.Write(metadata, discussion)\n}\n\n// Delete removes a trail from the entire/trails branch.\nfunc (s *Store) Delete(trailID ID) error {\n\tref, entries, err := s.getBranchEntries()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get branch entries: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tbasePath := trailID.Path() + \"/\"\n\n\t// Remove entries for this trail\n\tfound := false\n\tfor path := range entries {\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(path, basePath) {\n\t\t\tdelete(entries, path)\n\t\t\tfound = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif !found {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"trail %s not found\", trailID)\n\t}\n\n\t// Build tree and commit\n\tnewTreeHash, err := checkpoint.BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to build tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tauthorName, authorEmail := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)\n\tcommitMsg := fmt.Sprintf(\"Delete trail: %s\", trailID)\n\tcommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tnewRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName), commitHash)\n\tif err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to update branch reference: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// getBranchTree returns the tree for the entire/trails branch HEAD.\nfunc (s *Store) getBranchTree() (*object.Tree, error) {\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName)\n\tref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// Try remote tracking branch\n\t\tremoteRefName := plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName(\"origin\", paths.TrailsBranchName)\n\t\tref, err = s.repo.Reference(remoteRefName, true)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"trails branch not found: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tcommit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\ttree, err := commit.Tree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn tree, nil\n}\n\n// getBranchEntries returns the current branch reference and a flat map of all tree entries.\nfunc (s *Store) getBranchEntries() (*plumbing.Reference, map[string]object.TreeEntry, error) {\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.TrailsBranchName)\n\tref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"trails branch not found: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tcommit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\ttree, err := commit.Tree()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tentries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)\n\tif err := checkpoint.FlattenTree(s.repo, tree, \"\", entries); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to flatten tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn ref, entries, nil\n}\n\n// createCommit creates a commit on the trails branch.\nfunc (s *Store) createCommit(treeHash, parentHash plumbing.Hash, message, authorName, authorEmail string) (plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tsig := object.Signature{\n\t\tName:  authorName,\n\t\tEmail: authorEmail,\n\t\tWhen:  now,\n\t}\n\n\tcommit := &object.Commit{\n\t\tTreeHash:  treeHash,\n\t\tAuthor:    sig,\n\t\tCommitter: sig,\n\t\tMessage:   message,\n\t}\n\n\tif parentHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {\n\t\tcommit.ParentHashes = []plumbing.Hash{parentHash}\n\t}\n\n\tobj := s.repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()\n\tif err := commit.Encode(obj); err != nil {\n\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to encode commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\thash, err := s.repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to store commit: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn hash, nil\n}\n"

err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"command %v failed: %v\\n%s\", args, err, out)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a file and commit\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, \"README.md\"), []byte(\"# Test\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to write file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tcommitCmds := [][]string{\n\t\t{\"git\", \"-C\", dir, \"add\", \".\"},\n\t\t{\"git\", \"-C\", dir, \"commit\", \"-m\", \"Initial commit\"},\n\t}\n\tfor _, args := range commitCmds {\n\t\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, args[0], args[1:]...)\n\t\tif out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"command %v failed: %v\\n%s\", args, err, out)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to open repo: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn repo\n}\n\nfunc TestStore_EnsureBranch(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewStore(repo)\n\n\t// First call should create the branch\n\tif err := store.EnsureBranch(); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"EnsureBranch() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Second call should be idempotent\n\tif err := store.EnsureBranch(); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"EnsureBranch() second call error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestStore_WriteAndRead(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewStore(repo)\n\n\ttrailID, err := GenerateID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GenerateID() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tnow := time.Now().Truncate(time.Second)\n\tmetadata := &Metadata{\n\t\tTrailID:     trailID,\n\t\tBranch:      \"feature/test\",\n\t\tBase:        \"main\",\n\t\tTitle:       \"Test trail\",\n\t\tDescription: \"A test trail\",\n\t\tStatus:      StatusDraft,\n\t\tAuthor:      \"tester\",\n\t\tAssignees:   []string{},\n\t\tLabels:      []string{\"test\"},\n\t\tCreatedAt:   now,\n\t\tUpdatedAt:   now,\n\t}\n\n\tdiscussion := &Discussion{Comments: []Comment{}}\n\n\tif err := store.Write(metadata, discussion); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Write() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Read it back\n\tgotMeta, gotDisc, err := store.Read(trailID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Read() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif gotMeta.TrailID != trailID {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() trail_id = %s, want %s\", gotMeta.TrailID, trailID)\n\t}\n\tif gotMeta.Branch != \"feature/test\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() branch = %q, want %q\", gotMeta.Branch, \"feature/test\")\n\t}\n\tif gotMeta.Title != \"Test trail\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() title = %q, want %q\", gotMeta.Title, \"Test trail\")\n\t}\n\tif gotMeta.Status != StatusDraft {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() status = %q, want %q\", gotMeta.Status, StatusDraft)\n\t}\n\tif len(gotMeta.Labels) != 1 || gotMeta.Labels[0] != \"test\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() labels = %v, want [test]\", gotMeta.Labels)\n\t}\n\tif gotDisc == nil {\n\t\tt.Error(\"Read() discussion should not be nil\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestStore_FindByBranch(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewStore(repo)\n\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\n\t// Create two trails for different branches\n\tfor _, branch := range []string{\"feature/a\", \"feature/b\"} {\n\t\tid, err := GenerateID()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GenerateID() error = %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tmeta := &Metadata{\n\t\t\tTrailID:   id,\n\t\t\tBranch:    branch,\n\t\t\tBase:      \"main\",\n\t\t\tTitle:     HumanizeBranchName(branch),\n\t\t\tStatus:    StatusDraft,\n\t\t\tAuthor:    \"test\",\n\t\t\tAssignees: []string{},\n\t\t\tLabels:    []string{},\n\t\t\tCreatedAt: now,\n\t\t\tUpdatedAt: now,\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := store.Write(meta, nil); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Write() error = %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Find by branch\n\tfound, err := store.FindByBranch(\"feature/a\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"FindByBranch() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif found == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"FindByBranch() returned nil, expected trail\")\n\t}\n\tif found.Branch != \"feature/a\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"FindByBranch() branch = %q, want %q\", found.Branch, \"feature/a\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Not found\n\tnotFound, err := store.FindByBranch(\"feature/c\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"FindByBranch() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif notFound != nil {\n\t\tt.Error(\"FindByBranch() should return nil for non-existent branch\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestStore_List(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewStore(repo)\n\n\t// List when no trails exist (branch doesn't exist yet)\n\ttrails, err := store.List()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"List() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif trails != nil {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"List() = %v, want nil for empty store\", trails)\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a trail\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tid, err := GenerateID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GenerateID() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tmeta := &Metadata{\n\t\tTrailID:   id,\n\t\tBranch:    \"feature/test\",\n\t\tBase:      \"main\",\n\t\tTitle:     \"Test\",\n\t\tStatus:    StatusDraft,\n\t\tAuthor:    \"test\",\n\t\tAssignees: []string{},\n\t\tLabels:    []string{},\n\t\tCreatedAt: now,\n\t\tUpdatedAt: now,\n\t}\n\tif err := store.Write(meta, nil); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Write() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\ttrails, err = store.List()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"List() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(trails) != 1 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"List() returned %d trails, want 1\", len(trails))\n\t}\n\tif trails[0].TrailID != id {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"List()[0].TrailID = %s, want %s\", trails[0].TrailID, id)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestStore_Update(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewStore(repo)\n\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tid, err := GenerateID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GenerateID() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tmeta := &Metadata{\n\t\tTrailID:   id,\n\t\tBranch:    \"feature/test\",\n\t\tBase:      \"main\",\n\t\tTitle:     \"Original\",\n\t\tStatus:    StatusDraft,\n\t\tAuthor:    \"test\",\n\t\tAssignees: []string{},\n\t\tLabels:    []string{},\n\t\tCreatedAt: now,\n\t\tUpdatedAt: now,\n\t}\n\tif err := store.Write(meta, nil); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Write() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Update\n\tif err := store.Update(id, func(m *Metadata) {\n\t\tm.Title = \"Updated\"\n\t\tm.Status = StatusInProgress\n\t\tm.Labels = []string{\"urgent\"}\n\t}); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Update() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify\n\tupdated, _, err := store.Read(id)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Read() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif updated.Title != \"Updated\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() title = %q, want %q\", updated.Title, \"Updated\")\n\t}\n\tif updated.Status != StatusInProgress {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() status = %q, want %q\", updated.Status, StatusInProgress)\n\t}\n\tif len(updated.Labels) != 1 || updated.Labels[0] != \"urgent\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"Read() labels = %v, want [urgent]\", updated.Labels)\n\t}\n\tif !updated.UpdatedAt.After(now) {\n\t\tt.Error(\"Read() updated_at should be after original\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestStore_Delete(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewStore(repo)\n\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tid, err := GenerateID()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"GenerateID() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tmeta := &Metadata{\n\t\tTrailID:   id,\n\t\tBranch:    \"feature/test\",\n\t\tBase:      \"main\",\n\t\tTitle:     \"To delete\",\n\t\tStatus:    StatusDraft,\n\t\tAuthor:    \"test\",\n\t\tAssignees: []string{},\n\t\tLabels:    []string{},\n\t\tCreatedAt: now,\n\t\tUpdatedAt: now,\n\t}\n\tif err := store.Write(meta, nil); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Write() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Delete\n\tif err := store.Delete(id); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Delete() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify it's gone\n\t_, _, err = store.Read(id)\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tt.Error(\"Read() should fail after delete\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestStore_ReadNonExistent(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\trepo := initTestRepo(t)\n\tstore := NewStore(repo)\n\n\tif err := store.EnsureBranch(); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"EnsureBranch() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t_, _, err := store.Read(ID(\"abcdef123456\"))\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tt.Error(\"Read() should fail for non-existent trail\")\n\t}\n}\n"
}

    27→
    28→Running 'entire trail' without a subcommand shows the trail for the current

    42→// runTrailShow shows the trail for the current branch, or falls through to list.
    43→func runTrailShow(w io.Writer) error {
    44→	branch, err := GetCurrentBranch()
    45→	if err != nil {
    46→		return runTrailListAll(w, "", false)
    47→	}
    48→
    49→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
    50→	if err != nil {
    51→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	store := trail.NewTrailStore(repo)
    55→	metadata, err := store.FindByBranch(branch)
    56→	if err != nil || metadata == nil {
    57→		return runTrailListAll(w, "", false)
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	printTrailDetails(w, metadata)
    61→	return nil
    62→}
    63→
    64→func printTrailDetails(w io.Writer, m *trail.TrailMetadata) {
    65→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Trail: %s\n", m.Title)
    66→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  ID:      %s\n", m.TrailID)
    67→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Branch:  %s\n", m.Branch)
    68→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Base:    %s\n", m.Base)
    69→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Status:  %s\n", m.Status)
    70→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Author:  %s\n", m.Author)
    71→	if m.Description != "" {
    72→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Description: %s\n", m.Description)
    73→	}
    74→	if len(m.Labels) > 0 {
    75→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Labels:  %s\n", strings.Join(m.Labels, ", "))
    76→	}
    77→	if len(m.Assignees) > 0 {
    78→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Assignees: %s\n", strings.Join(m.Assignees, ", "))
    79→	}
    80→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Created: %s\n", m.CreatedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
    81→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Updated: %s\n", m.UpdatedAt.Format(time.RFC3339))
    82→}

   101→
   102→func runTrailListAll(w io.Writer, statusFilter string, jsonOutput bool) error {
   103→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
   104→	if err != nil {
   105→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	store := trail.NewTrailStore(repo)
   109→	trails, err := store.List()
   110→	if err != nil {
   111→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list trails: %w", err)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	if trails == nil {
   115→		trails = []*trail.TrailMetadata{}
   116→	}
   117→
   118→	// Apply status filter
   119→	if statusFilter != "" {
   120→		status := trail.TrailStatus(statusFilter)
   121→		if !status.IsValid() {
   122→			return fmt.Errorf("invalid status %q: valid values are %s", statusFilter, formatValidStatuses())
   123→		}
   124→		var filtered []*trail.TrailMetadata
   125→		for _, t := range trails {
   126→			if t.Status == status {
   127→				filtered = append(filtered, t)
   128→			}
   129→		}
   130→		trails = filtered
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	// Sort by updated_at descending
   134→	sort.Slice(trails, func(i, j int) bool {
   135→		return trails[i].UpdatedAt.After(trails[j].UpdatedAt)
   136→	})
   137→
   138→	if jsonOutput {
   139→		enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
   140→		enc.SetIndent("", "  ")
   141→		return enc.Encode(trails)
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	if len(trails) == 0 {
   145→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "No trails found.")
   146→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Create one with: entire trail create")
   147→		return nil
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	// Table output
   151→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "%-30s %-40s %-13s %-15s %s\n", "BRANCH", "TITLE", "STATUS", "AUTHOR", "UPDATED")
   152→	for _, t := range trails {
   153→		branch := truncate(t.Branch, 30)
   154→		title := truncate(t.Title, 40)
   155→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "%-30s %-40s %-13s %-15s %s\n",
   156→			branch, title, t.Status, truncate(t.Author, 15), timeAgo(t.UpdatedAt))
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	return nil
   160→}
   161→
   162→func newTrailCreateCmd() *cobra.Command {
   163→	var title, description, base, branch, status string
   164→
   165→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   166→		Use:   "create",
   167→		Short: "Create a trail for the current or a new branch",
   168→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   169→			return runTrailCreate(cmd.OutOrStdout(), cmd.ErrOrStderr(), title, description, base, branch, status)
   170→		},
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&title, "title", "", "Trail title")
   174→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&description, "description", "", "Trail description")
   175→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&base, "base", "", "Base branch (defaults to detected default branch)")
   176→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&branch, "branch", "", "Branch for the trail (defaults to current branch)")
   177→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&status, "status", "", "Initial status (defaults to draft)")
   178→
   179→	return cmd
   180→}
   181→
   182→func runTrailCreate(w, errW io.Writer, title, description, base, branch, statusStr string) error {
   183→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	// Determine branch
   189→	if branch == "" {
   190→		branch, err = GetCurrentBranch()
   191→		if err != nil {
   192→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to determine current branch: %w", err)
   193→		}
   194→	}
   195→
   196→	// Check if on default branch
   197→	isDefault, _, _ := IsOnDefaultBranch()
   198→	if isDefault && branch == "" {
   199→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Cannot create a trail on the default branch.")
   200→		fmt.Fprintln(errW, "Create or switch to a feature branch first.")
   201→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("cannot create trail on default branch"))
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	// Determine base branch
   205→	if base == "" {
   206→		base = strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo)
   207→		if base == "" {
   208→			base = "main"
   209→		}
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	// Check if trail already exists for this branch
   213→	store := trail.NewTrailStore(repo)
   214→	existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branch)
   215→	if err == nil && existing != nil {
   216→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Trail already exists for branch %q (ID: %s)\n", branch, existing.TrailID)
   217→		return nil
   218→	}
   219→
   220→	// Determine title
   221→	if title == "" {
   222→		defaultTitle := trail.HumanizeBranchName(branch)
   223→
   224→		// Interactive mode if flags not provided
   225→		if !hasFlag("title") {
   226→			var inputTitle string
   227→			form := NewAccessibleForm(
   228→				huh.NewGroup(
   229→					huh.NewInput().
   230→						Title("Trail title").
   231→						Placeholder(defaultTitle).
   232→						Value(&inputTitle),
   233→				),
   234→			)
   235→			if formErr := form.Run(); formErr != nil {
   236→				return fmt.Errorf("form cancelled: %w", formErr)
   237→			}
   238→			if inputTitle != "" {
   239→				title = inputTitle
   240→			} else {
   241→				title = defaultTitle
   242→			}
   243→		} else {
   244→			title = defaultTitle
   245→		}
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	// Determine status
   249→	var trailStatus trail.TrailStatus
   250→	if statusStr != "" {
   251→		trailStatus = trail.TrailStatus(statusStr)
   252→		if !trailStatus.IsValid() {
   253→			return fmt.Errorf("invalid status %q: valid values are %s", statusStr, formatValidStatuses())
   254→		}
   255→	} else {
   256→		trailStatus = trail.StatusDraft
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	// Generate trail ID
   260→	trailID, err := trail.GenerateID()
   261→	if err != nil {
   262→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate trail ID: %w", err)
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Get git author
   266→	authorName, _ := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   267→
   268→	now := time.Now()
   269→	metadata := &trail.TrailMetadata{
   270→		TrailID:     trailID,
   271→		Branch:      branch,
   272→		Base:        base,
   273→		Title:       title,
   274→		Description: description,
   275→		Status:      trailStatus,
   276→		Author:      authorName,
   277→		Assignees:   []string{},
   278→		Labels:      []string{},
   279→		CreatedAt:   now,
   280→		UpdatedAt:   now,
   281→	}
   282→
   283→	if err := store.Write(metadata, nil); err != nil {
   284→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create trail: %w", err)
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Created trail %q for branch %s (ID: %s)\n", title, branch, trailID)
   288→	return nil
   289→}
   290→
   291→func newTrailUpdateCmd() *cobra.Command {
   292→	var statusStr, title, description, branch string
   293→	var labelAdd, labelRemove []string
   294→
   295→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
   296→		Use:   "update",
   297→		Short: "Update trail metadata",
   298→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   299→			return runTrailUpdate(cmd.OutOrStdout(), statusStr, title, description, branch, labelAdd, labelRemove)
   300→		},
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&statusStr, "status", "", "Update status")
   304→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&title, "title", "", "Update title")
   305→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&description, "description", "", "Update description")
   306→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&branch, "branch", "", "Branch to update trail for (defaults to current)")
   307→	cmd.Flags().StringSliceVar(&labelAdd, "add-label", nil, "Add label(s)")
   308→	cmd.Flags().StringSliceVar(&labelRemove, "remove-label", nil, "Remove label(s)")
   309→
   310→	return cmd
   311→}
   312→
   313→func runTrailUpdate(w io.Writer, statusStr, title, description, branch string, labelAdd, labelRemove []string) error {
   314→	repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
   315→	if err != nil {
   316→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	// Determine branch
   320→	if branch == "" {
   321→		branch, err = GetCurrentBranch()
   322→		if err != nil {
   323→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to determine current branch: %w", err)
   324→		}
   325→	}
   326→
   327→	store := trail.NewTrailStore(repo)
   328→	metadata, err := store.FindByBranch(branch)
   329→	if err != nil {
   330→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to find trail: %w", err)
   331→	}
   332→	if metadata == nil {
   333→		return fmt.Errorf("no trail found for branch %q", branch)
   334→	}
   335→
   336→	// Validate status if provided
   337→	if statusStr != "" {
   338→		status := trail.TrailStatus(statusStr)
   339→		if !status.IsValid() {
   340→			return fmt.Errorf("invalid status %q: valid values are %s", statusStr, formatValidStatuses())
   341→		}
   342→	}
   343→
   344→	err = store.Update(metadata.TrailID, func(m *trail.TrailMetadata) {
   345→		if statusStr != "" {
   346→			m.Status = trail.TrailStatus(statusStr)
   347→		}
   348→		if title != "" {
   349→			m.Title = title
   350→		}
   351→		if description != "" {
   352→			m.Description = description
   353→		}
   354→		for _, l := range labelAdd {
   355→			if !containsString(m.Labels, l) {
   356→				m.Labels = append(m.Labels, l)
   357→			}
   358→		}
   359→		for _, l := range labelRemove {
   360→			m.Labels = removeString(m.Labels, l)
   361→		}
   362→	})
   363→	if err != nil {
   364→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update trail: %w", err)
   365→	}
   366→
   367→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Updated trail for branch %s\n", branch)
   368→	return nil
   369→}
   370→
   371→// AutoCreateTrail creates a trail automatically for the current branch if one doesn't exist.
   372→// This is called during session start for non-main branches.
   373→func AutoCreateTrail(repo *git.Repository, branchName, baseBranch string) error {
   374→	store := trail.NewTrailStore(repo)
   375→
   376→	existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)
   377→	if err == nil && existing != nil {
   378→		return nil // Trail already exists
   379→	}
   380→
   381→	trailID, err := trail.GenerateID()
   382→	if err != nil {
   383→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate trail ID: %w", err)
   384→	}
   385→
   386→	authorName, _ := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   387→	now := time.Now()
   388→
   389→	metadata := &trail.TrailMetadata{
   390→		TrailID:   trailID,
   391→		Branch:    branchName,
   392→		Base:      baseBranch,
   393→		Title:     trail.HumanizeBranchName(branchName),
   394→		Status:    trail.StatusInProgress,
   395→		Author:    authorName,
   396→		Assignees: []string{},
   397→		Labels:    []string{},
   398→		CreatedAt: now,
   399→		UpdatedAt: now,
   400→	}
   401→
   402→	if err := store.Write(metadata, nil); err != nil {
   403→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create trail: %w", err)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created trail for branch: %s\n", branchName)
   407→	return nil
   408→}
   409→
   410→// hasFlag is a simple helper that checks os.Args for --flag presence.
   411→// Used to distinguish between "flag not provided" and "flag provided with empty value".
   412→func hasFlag(name string) bool {
   413→	for _, arg := range os.Args {
   414→		if arg == "--"+name || strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--"+name+"=") {
   415→			return true
   416→		}
   417→	}
   418→	return false
   419→}
   420→
   421→func truncate(s string, maxLen int) string {
   422→	if len(s) <= maxLen {
   423→		return s
   424→	}
   425→	if maxLen <= 3 {
   426→		return s[:maxLen]
   427→	}
   428→	return s[:maxLen-3] + "..."
   429→}
   430→
   431→func formatValidStatuses() string {
   432→	statuses := trail.ValidStatuses()
   433→	names := make([]string, len(statuses))
   434→	for i, s := range statuses {
   435→		names[i] = string(s)
   436→	}
   437→	return strings.Join(names, ", ")
   438→}
   439→
   440→func containsString(slice []string, s string) bool {
   441→	for _, v := range slice {
   442→		if v == s {
   443→			return true
   444→		}
   445→	}
   446→	return false
   447→}
   448→
   449→func removeString(slice []string, s string) []string {
   450→	var result []string
   451→	for _, v := range slice {
   452→		if v != s {
   453→			result = append(result, v)
   454→		}
   455→	}
   456→	return result
   457→}

> AGENT

The `gosec` linter uses `G104` and the `//nolint:errcheck` directive only suppresses the `errcheck` linter, not `gosec`. I need to add `gosec` to the nolint:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01TDYaBDerrP8SYU6B9LGvjL
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/trail/store.go",
  "old_string": "\t\t\t//nolint:errcheck // best-effort decode of optional discussion\n\t\t\t\tjson.NewDecoder(discussionReader).Decode(&discussion)",
  "new_string": "\t\t\t//nolint:errcheck,gosec // best-effort decode of optional discussion\n\t\t\t\tjson.NewDecoder(discussionReader).Decode(&discussion)"
}
```

id: toolu_01HaGKtkdk5K4Qobn74zUR12
```
   127→- **75 tokens** (lint/CI) - Blocks on serious duplication (~20+ lines)
   128→- **50 tokens** (dup) - Advisory, catches smaller patterns (~10+ lines)
   129→
   130→When duplication is found:
   131→1. Check if a helper already exists in `common.go` or nearby utility files
   132→2. If not, consider extracting the duplicated logic to a shared helper
   133→3. If duplication is intentional (e.g., test setup), add a `//nolint:dupl` comment with explanation
   134→
   135→## Code Patterns
   136→
   137→### Error Handling
   138→
   139→The CLI uses a specific pattern for error output to avoid duplication between Cobra and main.go.
   140→
   141→**How it works:**
   142→- `root.go` sets `SilenceErrors: true` globally - Cobra never prints errors
   143→- `main.go` prints errors to stderr, unless the error is a `SilentError`
   144→- Commands return `NewSilentError(err)` when they've already printed a custom message
   145→
   146→**When to use `SilentError`:**
   147→Use `NewSilentError()` when you want to print a custom, user-friendly error message instead of the raw error:
   148→
   149→```go
   150→// In a command's RunE function:
   151→if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
   152→    cmd.SilenceUsage = true  // Don't show usage for prerequisite errors
   153→    fmt.Fprintln(cmd.ErrOrStderr(), "Not a git repository. Please run 'entire enable' from within a git repository.")
   154→    return NewSilentError(errors.New("not a git repository"))
   155→}
   156→```
```

```
   341→#### Checkpoint Package (`cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/`)
   342→- `checkpoint.go` - Data types (`Checkpoint`, `TemporaryCheckpoint`, `CommittedCheckpoint`)
   343→- `store.go` - `GitStore` struct wrapping git repository
   344→- `temporary.go` - Shadow branch operations (`WriteTemporary`, `ReadTemporary`, `ListTemporary`)
   345→- `committed.go` - Metadata branch operations (`WriteCommitted`, `ReadCommitted`, `ListCommitted`)
   346→
   347→#### Session Package (`cmd/entire/cli/session/`)
   348→- `session.go` - Session data types and interfaces
   349→- `state.go` - `StateStore` for managing `.git/entire-sessions/` files
   350→- `phase.go` - Session phase state machine (phases, events, transitions, actions)
   351→
   352→#### Session Phase State Machine
   353→
   354→Sessions track their lifecycle through phases managed by a state machine in `session/phase.go`:
   355→
   356→**Phases:** `ACTIVE`, `IDLE`, `ENDED`
   357→
   358→**Events:**
   359→- `TurnStart` - Agent begins a turn (UserPromptSubmit hook)
   360→- `TurnEnd` - Agent finishes a turn (Stop hook)
   361→- `GitCommit` - A git commit was made (PostCommit hook)
   362→- `SessionStart` - New session started
   363→- `SessionStop` - Session explicitly stopped
   364→
   365→**Key transitions:**
   366→- `IDLE + TurnStart → ACTIVE` - Agent starts working
   367→- `ACTIVE + TurnEnd → IDLE` - Agent finishes turn
   368→- `ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE` - User commits while agent is working (condense immediately)
   369→- `IDLE + GitCommit → IDLE` - User commits between turns (condense immediately)
   370→- `ENDED + GitCommit → ENDED` - Post-session commit (condense if files touched)
```

  "new_string": "#### Trail Package (`cmd/entire/cli/trail/`)\n- `trail.go` - Types (`ID`, `Status`, `Metadata`, `Discussion`, `Comment`) and `HumanizeBranchName()` helper\n- `store.go` - `Store` struct with CRUD operations on the `entire/trails` orphan branch\n\n**Command:** `entire trail` — manage trails for branches\n- `entire trail` — show current branch's trail, or list all\n- `entire trail list` — list all trails (`--status`, `--json` flags)\n- `entire trail create` — create a trail (interactive or via `--title`, `--branch`, `--status` flags)\n- `entire trail update` — update trail metadata (`--status`, `--title`, `--add-label`, `--remove-label` flags)\n\n**Auto-create:** On session TurnStart, if on a non-main branch without a trail, one is auto-created with `in_progress` status.\n\n**Push:** Pre-push hooks push `entire/trails` branch alongside `entire/checkpoints/v1`.\n\n**Storage:** `entire/trails` orphan branch with sharded paths: `<trail_id[:2]>/<trail_id[2:]>/metadata.json`\n\n**Status lifecycle:** `draft → open → in_progress → in_review → done → closed`\n\n#### Session Package (`cmd/entire/cli/session/`)"
}
```

## Summary of Codebase Research for `entire trail enable/disable` Implementation

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here are all the relevant code patterns, file paths, and function signatures you'll need to implement the `entire trail enable/disable` subcommands.

### 1. **Settings Storage and Loading Pattern**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings.go`

**Key Struct:**
```go
type EntireSettings struct {
    Strategy        string         `json:"strategy"`
    Enabled         bool           `json:"enabled"`
    LocalDev        bool           `json:"local_dev,omitempty"`
    LogLevel        string         `json:"log_level,omitempty"`
    StrategyOptions map[string]any `json:"strategy_options,omitempty"`
    Telemetry       *bool          `json:"telemetry,omitempty"`
}
```

**Loading Functions:**
- `Load() (*EntireSettings, error)` - Load base settings + local overrides
- `LoadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error)` - Load from specific file
- `Save(settings *EntireSettings) error` - Save to `.entire/settings.json`
- `SaveLocal(settings *EntireSettings) error` - Save to `.entire/settings.local.json`

**Merging Pattern:** The `mergeJSON` function shows how to merge local settings overrides. It checks `raw` map for specific keys and carefully merges nested structures.

---

### 2. **Settings Checker Pattern (Package-Level Convenience Functions)**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings.go`

**Two-Level Pattern for Feature Toggles:**

```go
// Package-level function (global check)
func IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
    settings, err := Load()
    if err != nil {
        return false // Default: false on error
    }
    return settings.IsSummarizeEnabled()
}

// Instance-level method (on loaded settings)
func (s *EntireSettings) IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
    if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
        return false
    }
    summarizeOpts, ok := s.StrategyOptions["summarize"].(map[string]any)
    if !ok {
        return false
    }
    enabled, ok := summarizeOpts["enabled"].(bool)
    if !ok {
        return false
    }
    return enabled
}
```

**Simpler Boolean Flag Pattern (for push_sessions):**

```go
func (s *EntireSettings) IsPushSessionsDisabled() bool {
    if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
        return false
    }
    val, exists := s.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"]
    if !exists {
        return false
    }
    if boolVal, ok := val.(bool); ok {
        return !boolVal // disabled = !push_sessions
    }
    return false
}
```

**Key Notes:**
- Use `StrategyOptions` map for nested settings
- Default to `false` on missing keys or errors
- For boolean flags like `push_sessions`, store directly in `StrategyOptions` (not nested)
- For complex settings like `summarize`, use nested `map[string]any`

### 3. **Command-Level Enable/Disable Pattern**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/setup.go`

**Simple Enable Function (reference):**
```go
func runEnable(w io.Writer) error {
    settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
    }

    settings.Enabled = true
    if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
    }

    fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now enabled.")
    return nil
}
```

**Simple Disable Function (reference):**
```go
func runDisable(w io.Writer, useProjectSettings bool) error {
    settings, err := LoadEntireSettings()
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to load settings: %w", err)
    }

    settings.Enabled = false

    // If --project flag is specified, always write to project settings
    if useProjectSettings {
        if err := SaveEntireSettings(settings); err != nil {
            return fmt.Errorf("failed to save settings: %w", err)
        }
    } else {
        // Always write to local settings file (create if doesn't exist)
        if err := SaveEntireSettingsLocal(settings); err != nil {
            return fmt.Errorf("failed to save local settings: %w", err)
        }
    }

    fmt.Fprintln(w, "Entire is now disabled.")
    return nil
}
```

### 5. **Trail Auto-Creation Lifecycle**

**File:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/trail_cmd.go` (lines 378-415)

**Auto-Create Function:**
```go
// AutoCreateTrail creates a trail automatically for the current branch if one doesn't exist.
// This is called during session start for non-main branches.
func AutoCreateTrail(repo *git.Repository, branchName, baseBranch string) error {
    store := trail.NewStore(repo)

    existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)
    if err == nil && existing != nil {
        return nil // Trail already exists
    }

    trailID, err := trail.GenerateID()
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate trail ID: %w", err)
    }

    authorName, _ := checkpoint.GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
    now := time.Now()

    metadata := &trail.Metadata{
        TrailID:   trailID,
        Branch:    branchName,
        Base:      baseBranch,
        Title:     trail.HumanizeBranchName(branchName),
        Status:    trail.StatusInProgress,
        Author:    authorName,
        Assignees: []string{},
        Labels:    []string{},
        CreatedAt: now,
        UpdatedAt: now,
    }

    if err := store.Write(metadata, nil); err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("failed to create trail: %w", err)
    }

    fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created trail for branch: %s\n", branchName)
    return nil
}
```

**Called From:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go` (line 148)

```go
// autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart creates a trail if on a non-main branch without one.
// Non-blocking: logs errors but doesn't fail the session.
func autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart() {
    isDefault, branchName, err := IsOnDefaultBranch()
    if err != nil || isDefault || branchName == "" {
        return
    }

    repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository()
    if err != nil {
        return
    }

    baseBranch := strategy.GetDefaultBranchName(repo)
    if baseBranch == "" {
        baseBranch = defaultBaseBranch
    }

    if err := AutoCreateTrail(repo, branchName, baseBranch); err != nil {
        fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to auto-create trail: %v\n", err)
    }
}
```

**Call location in lifecycle:** At turn start (UserPromptSubmit hook), line 148 in `lifecycle.go`

// PrePush is called by the git pre-push hook before pushing to a remote.
// It pushes the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch alongside the user's push.
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrePush(remote string) error {
    if err := pushSessionsBranchCommon(remote, paths.MetadataBranchName); err != nil {
        return err
    }
    return PushTrailsBranch(remote)
}
```

**Shared Push Logic:** `/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/push_common.go` (lines 27-79)

```go
// pushSessionsBranchCommon is the shared implementation for pushing session branches.
// Used by both manual-commit and auto-commit strategies.
func pushSessionsBranchCommon(remote, branchName string) error {
    // Check if pushing is disabled
    if isPushSessionsDisabled() {
        return nil
    }

    repo, err := OpenRepository()
    if err != nil {
        return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
    }

    // Check if branch exists locally
    branchRef := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName)
    localRef, err := repo.Reference(branchRef, true)
    if err != nil {
        // No branch, nothing to push
        return nil //nolint:nilerr // Expected when no sessions exist yet
    }

    // Check if there's actually something to push
    if !hasUnpushedSessionsCommon(repo, remote, localRef.Hash(), branchName) {
        // Nothing to push - skip silently
        return nil
    }

    return doPushSessionsBranch(remote, branchName)
}

// isPushSessionsDisabled checks if push_sessions is disabled in settings.
func isPushSessionsDisabled() bool {
    s, err := settings.Load()
    if err != nil {
        return false // Default: push is enabled
    }
    return s.IsPushSessionsDisabled()
}
```

```
     1→# Plan: `entire trail` command
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→PR #738 in entire.io introduces a "Trails" system on the web — a branch-centric work tracking abstraction stored on an `entire/trails` orphan branch. Trails answer "why/what" (human intent) while Checkpoints answer "how/when" (machine snapshots). They're linked by branch name.
     6→
     7→This plan adds the CLI counterpart: an `entire trail` command that creates and manages trail metadata locally in git. When pushed (via pre-push hook), the web UI picks it up via webhooks automatically. Additionally, trails are auto-created when an agent session starts on a non-main branch.
     8→
     9→## Command Structure
    10→
    11→```
    12→entire trail              # Show current branch's trail (or list if none)
    13→entire trail list          # List all trails (with optional --status filter)
    14→entire trail create        # Create a trail for current or new branch
    15→entire trail update        # Update trail metadata (status, title, etc.)
    16→```
    17→
    18→## Key Design Decisions
    19→
    20→- **Storage**: Dedicated `entire/trails` orphan branch (matches web PR)
    21→- **ID format**: 12 hex char IDs with `<id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/` sharding (same as checkpoints)
    22→- **Auto-create**: On session start (UserPromptSubmit hook), if on a non-main branch without a trail, auto-create one with status `in_progress`
    23→- **Push**: Pre-push hook pushes `entire/trails` alongside `entire/checkpoints/v1`
    24→- **Linking**: Trail ↔ Checkpoints linked by branch name (no foreign key)
    25→
    26→## Data Format
    27→
    28→### `entire/trails` branch structure
    29→```
    30→<trail_id[:2]>/<trail_id[2:]>/
    31→  metadata.json
    32→  discussion.json    # Empty initially, comments come later
    33→```
    34→
    35→### `metadata.json` (matches web PR format)
    36→```json
    37→{
    38→  "trail_id": "abcdef123456",
    39→  "branch": "feature/auth",
    40→  "base": "main",
    41→  "title": "Add user authentication",
    42→  "description": "",
    43→  "status": "in_progress",
    44→  "author": "alice",
    45→  "assignees": [],
    46→  "labels": [],
    47→  "created_at": "2026-02-18T10:30:00Z",
    48→  "updated_at": "2026-02-20T14:22:00Z"
    49→}
    50→```
    51→
    52→### Trail Status Lifecycle
    53→```
    54→draft → open → in_progress → in_review → done → closed
    55→```
    56→
    57→## Implementation Steps
    58→
    59→### 1. Add `TrailsBranchName` constant
    60→**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/paths/paths.go` (line 38)
    61→
    62→Add `TrailsBranchName = "entire/trails"` alongside existing `MetadataBranchName`.
    63→
    64→### 2. Create trail package
    65→**New directory**: `cmd/entire/cli/trail/`
    66→
    67→#### `trail.go` — Types and helpers
    68→- `TrailID` type (12 hex chars, same pattern as `checkpoint/id.CheckpointID` in `cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/id/id.go`)
    69→  - `Generate()`, `Validate()`, `Path()`, `String()`, `IsEmpty()`
    70→- `TrailStatus` type with constants: `StatusDraft`, `StatusOpen`, `StatusInProgress`, `StatusInReview`, `StatusDone`, `StatusClosed`
    71→  - `ValidStatuses()` and `IsValid()` for validation
    72→- `TrailMetadata` struct matching web PR format (trail_id, branch, base, title, description, status, author, assignees, labels, created_at, updated_at, merged_at)
    73→- `TrailDiscussion` struct (initially `Comments []TrailComment` — empty, ready for future use)
    74→- `TrailComment` and `TrailCommentReply` structs (matching web PR)
    75→- `HumanizeBranchName(branch string) string` helper
    76→  - Strip common prefixes (`feature/`, `fix/`, `bugfix/`, `chore/`, `hotfix/`, `release/`)
    77→  - Replace `-`/`_` with spaces, capitalize first word
    78→
    79→#### `store.go` — Git storage operations
    80→`TrailStore` wrapping `*git.Repository`:
    81→- `NewTrailStore(repo)` constructor

    83→  - Follow pattern from `checkpoint.GitStore.ensureSessionsBranch()` (`cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/committed.go:1264`)
    84→- `Write(ctx, metadata, discussion)` — write metadata.json + discussion.json to sharded path
    85→- `Read(ctx, trailID)` — read a single trail by ID

    87→- `List(ctx)` — list all trails

    97→### 3. Create trail command
    98→**New file**: `cmd/entire/cli/trail_cmd.go`
    99→
   100→#### `entire trail` (root, no subcommand)
   101→- If on a branch with a trail → show trail details (title, status, branch, base, timestamps)
   102→- If no trail for current branch → fall through to `entire trail list` output
   103→
   104→#### `entire trail list`
   105→- Scan `entire/trails` branch, list all trails
   106→- Flags: `--status <status>` (filter), `--json` (JSON output)
   107→- Table output: branch, title, status, author, created_at
   108→
   109→#### `entire trail create`
   110→- Interactive mode (default): prompt for title, description, base branch
   111→  - Title defaults to `HumanizeBranchName(currentBranch)`
   112→  - Base defaults to detected default branch — reuse `strategy.GetDefaultBranchName()` (`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/common.go:1447`)
   113→  - Uses `NewAccessibleForm()` for accessibility (`cmd/entire/cli/utils.go`)
   114→- Non-interactive flags: `--title`, `--description`, `--base`, `--branch`, `--status`
   115→  - If `--branch` specified and branch doesn't exist, create it via `git checkout -b`
   116→  - If no `--branch`, use current branch via `GetCurrentBranch()` (`cmd/entire/cli/git_operations.go:156`)
   117→  - Error if on main/master — use `IsOnDefaultBranch()` (`cmd/entire/cli/git_operations.go:83`)
   118→- Initial status: `StatusDraft` for explicit create, `StatusInProgress` for auto-create
   119→
   120→#### `entire trail update`
   121→- Requires being on a branch with a trail (or `--branch` flag)
   122→- Flags: `--status`, `--title`, `--description`, `--label add:X`, `--label remove:X`
   123→- Updates `updated_at` timestamp automatically
   124→
   125→### 4. Auto-create trail on session start
   126→**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go` — in `handleLifecycleTurnStart()` (line 112)
   127→
   128→Insert after session initialization (line ~145), before return:
   129→1. Get current branch via `GetCurrentBranch()` (`cmd/entire/cli/git_operations.go:156`)
   130→2. Check if on default branch via `IsOnDefaultBranch()` — skip if true or error
   131→3. Open trail store, call `FindByBranch(ctx, branchName)`

     1→// Package settings provides configuration loading for Entire.
     2→// This package is separate from cli to allow strategy package to import it
     3→// without creating an import cycle (cli imports strategy).
     4→package settings
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"bytes"
     8→	"encoding/json"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"path/filepath"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    15→)
    16→
    17→// DefaultStrategyName is the default strategy when none is configured.
    18→// This is duplicated here to avoid importing the strategy package (which would create a cycle).
    19→const DefaultStrategyName = "manual-commit"
    20→
    21→const (
    22→	// EntireSettingsFile is the path to the Entire settings file
    23→	EntireSettingsFile = ".entire/settings.json"
    24→	// EntireSettingsLocalFile is the path to the local settings override file (not committed)
    25→	EntireSettingsLocalFile = ".entire/settings.local.json"
    26→)
    27→
    28→// EntireSettings represents the .entire/settings.json configuration
    29→type EntireSettings struct {
    30→	// Strategy is the name of the git strategy to use
    31→	Strategy string `json:"strategy"`
    32→
    33→	// Enabled indicates whether Entire is active. When false, CLI commands
    34→	// show a disabled message and hooks exit silently. Defaults to true.
    35→	Enabled bool `json:"enabled"`
    36→
    37→	// LocalDev indicates whether to use "go run" instead of the "entire" binary
    38→	// This is used for development when the binary is not installed
    39→	LocalDev bool `json:"local_dev,omitempty"`
    40→
    41→	// LogLevel sets the logging verbosity (debug, info, warn, error).
    42→	// Can be overridden by ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL environment variable.
    43→	// Defaults to "info".
    44→	LogLevel string `json:"log_level,omitempty"`
    45→
    46→	// StrategyOptions contains strategy-specific configuration
    47→	StrategyOptions map[string]any `json:"strategy_options,omitempty"`
    48→
    49→	// Telemetry controls anonymous usage analytics.
    50→	// nil = not asked yet (show prompt), true = opted in, false = opted out
    51→	Telemetry *bool `json:"telemetry,omitempty"`
    52→}
    53→
    54→// Load loads the Entire settings from .entire/settings.json,
    55→// then applies any overrides from .entire/settings.local.json if it exists.
    56→// Returns default settings if neither file exists.
    57→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
    58→func Load() (*EntireSettings, error) {
    59→	// Get absolute paths for settings files
    60→	settingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsFile)
    61→	if err != nil {
    62→		settingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsFile // Fallback to relative
    63→	}
    64→	localSettingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsLocalFile)
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		localSettingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsLocalFile // Fallback to relative
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	// Load base settings
    70→	settings, err := loadFromFile(settingsFileAbs)
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading settings file: %w", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	// Apply local overrides if they exist
    76→	localData, err := os.ReadFile(localSettingsFileAbs) //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		if !os.IsNotExist(err) {
    79→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading local settings file: %w", err)
    80→		}
    81→		// Local file doesn't exist, continue without overrides
    82→	} else {
    83→		if err := mergeJSON(settings, localData); err != nil {
    84→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("merging local settings: %w", err)
    85→		}
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	applyDefaults(settings)
    89→
    90→	return settings, nil
    91→}
    92→
    93→// LoadFromFile loads settings from a specific file path without merging local overrides.
    94→// Returns default settings if the file doesn't exist.
    95→// Use this when you need to display individual settings files separately.
    96→func LoadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
    97→	return loadFromFile(filePath)
    98→}
    99→
   100→// loadFromFile loads settings from a specific file path.
   101→// Returns default settings if the file doesn't exist.
   102→func loadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
   103→	settings := &EntireSettings{
   104→		Strategy: DefaultStrategyName,
   105→		Enabled:  true, // Default to enabled
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	data, err := os.ReadFile(filePath) //nolint:gosec // path is from caller
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   111→			return settings, nil
   112→		}
   113→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w", err)
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
   117→	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
   118→	if err := dec.Decode(settings); err != nil {
   119→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing settings file: %w", err)
   120→	}
   121→	applyDefaults(settings)
   122→
   123→	return settings, nil
   124→}
   125→
   126→// mergeJSON merges JSON data into existing settings.
   127→// Only non-zero values from the JSON override existing settings.
   128→func mergeJSON(settings *EntireSettings, data []byte) error {
   129→	// First, validate that there are no unknown keys using strict decoding
   130→	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
   131→	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
   132→	var temp EntireSettings
   133→	if err := dec.Decode(&temp); err != nil {
   134→		return fmt.Errorf("parsing JSON: %w", err)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Parse into a map to check which fields are present
   138→	var raw map[string]json.RawMessage
   139→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   140→		return fmt.Errorf("parsing JSON: %w", err)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	// Override strategy if present and non-empty
   144→	if strategyRaw, ok := raw["strategy"]; ok {
   145→		var s string
   146→		if err := json.Unmarshal(strategyRaw, &s); err != nil {
   147→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing strategy field: %w", err)
   148→		}
   149→		if s != "" {
   150→			settings.Strategy = s
   151→		}
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	// Override enabled if present
   155→	if enabledRaw, ok := raw["enabled"]; ok {
   156→		var e bool
   157→		if err := json.Unmarshal(enabledRaw, &e); err != nil {
   158→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing enabled field: %w", err)
   159→		}
   160→		settings.Enabled = e
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	// Override local_dev if present
   164→	if localDevRaw, ok := raw["local_dev"]; ok {
   165→		var ld bool
   166→		if err := json.Unmarshal(localDevRaw, &ld); err != nil {
   167→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing local_dev field: %w", err)
   168→		}
   169→		settings.LocalDev = ld
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	// Override log_level if present and non-empty
   173→	if logLevelRaw, ok := raw["log_level"]; ok {
   174→		var ll string
   175→		if err := json.Unmarshal(logLevelRaw, &ll); err != nil {
   176→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing log_level field: %w", err)
   177→		}
   178→		if ll != "" {
   179→			settings.LogLevel = ll
   180→		}
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	// Merge strategy_options if present
   184→	if optionsRaw, ok := raw["strategy_options"]; ok {
   185→		var opts map[string]any
   186→		if err := json.Unmarshal(optionsRaw, &opts); err != nil {
   187→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing strategy_options field: %w", err)
   188→		}
   189→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   190→			settings.StrategyOptions = opts
   191→		} else {
   192→			for k, v := range opts {
   193→				settings.StrategyOptions[k] = v
   194→			}
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Override telemetry if present
   199→	if telemetryRaw, ok := raw["telemetry"]; ok {
   200→		var t bool
   201→		if err := json.Unmarshal(telemetryRaw, &t); err != nil {
   202→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing telemetry field: %w", err)
   203→		}
   204→		settings.Telemetry = &t
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	return nil
   208→}
   209→
   210→func applyDefaults(settings *EntireSettings) {
   211→	if settings.Strategy == "" {
   212→		settings.Strategy = DefaultStrategyName
   213→	}
   214→}
   215→
   216→// IsSummarizeEnabled checks if auto-summarize is enabled in settings.
   217→// Returns false by default if settings cannot be loaded or the key is missing.
   218→func IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
   219→	settings, err := Load()
   220→	if err != nil {
   221→		return false
   222→	}
   223→	return settings.IsSummarizeEnabled()
   224→}
   225→
   226→// IsSummarizeEnabled checks if auto-summarize is enabled in this settings instance.
   227→func (s *EntireSettings) IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
   228→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   229→		return false
   230→	}
   231→	summarizeOpts, ok := s.StrategyOptions["summarize"].(map[string]any)
   232→	if !ok {
   233→		return false
   234→	}
   235→	enabled, ok := summarizeOpts["enabled"].(bool)
   236→	if !ok {
   237→		return false
   238→	}
   239→	return enabled
   240→}
   241→
   242→// IsPushSessionsDisabled checks if push_sessions is disabled in settings.
   243→// Returns true if push_sessions is explicitly set to false.
   244→func (s *EntireSettings) IsPushSessionsDisabled() bool {
   245→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   246→		return false
   247→	}
   248→	val, exists := s.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"]
   249→	if !exists {
   250→		return false
   251→	}
   252→	if boolVal, ok := val.(bool); ok {
   253→		return !boolVal // disabled = !push_sessions
   254→	}
   255→	return false
   256→}
   257→
   258→// Save saves the settings to .entire/settings.json.
   259→func Save(settings *EntireSettings) error {
   260→	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsFile)
   261→}
   262→
   263→// SaveLocal saves the settings to .entire/settings.local.json.
   264→func SaveLocal(settings *EntireSettings) error {
   265→	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsLocalFile)
   266→}
   267→
   268→// saveToFile saves settings to the specified file path.
   269→func saveToFile(settings *EntireSettings, filePath string) error {
   270→	// Get absolute path for the file
   271→	filePathAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(filePath)
   272→	if err != nil {
   273→		filePathAbs = filePath // Fallback to relative
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Ensure directory exists
   277→	dir := filepath.Dir(filePathAbs)
   278→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   279→		return fmt.Errorf("creating settings directory: %w", err)
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(settings, "", "  ")
   283→	if err != nil {
   284→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling settings: %w", err)
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	//nolint:gosec // G306: settings file is config, not secrets; 0o644 is appropriate
   288→	if err := os.WriteFile(filePathAbs, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   289→		return fmt.Errorf("writing settings file: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→	return nil
   292→}
   293→

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/.claude/plans/wondrous-finding-raven.md",
  "content": "# Plan: Add `entire trail enable` / `entire trail disable`\n\n## Context\n\nThe `entire trail` command was just implemented but lacks enable/disable subcommands. Users need a way to opt out of trail auto-creation and pushing. The feature should be enabled by default but togglable via settings.\n\n## Approach\n\nStore a `trails` boolean in `StrategyOptions` (same pattern as `push_sessions`). Default: enabled (true). When disabled, skip auto-create on session start and skip pushing the trails branch.\n\n## Implementation Steps\n\n### 1. Add settings convenience functions\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings.go`\n\nAdd `IsTrailsDisabled()` method on `*EntireSettings` — follows the `IsPushSessionsDisabled()` pattern exactly:\n```go\nfunc (s *EntireSettings) IsTrailsDisabled() bool {\n    // Check StrategyOptions[\"trails\"] — false means disabled\n    // Missing/nil means enabled (default)\n}\n```\n\nAdd package-level convenience:\n```go\nfunc IsTrailsDisabled() bool {\n    s, err := Load()\n    if err != nil { return false }\n    return s.IsTrailsDisabled()\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Add `enable` and `disable` subcommands\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/trail_cmd.go`\n\nAdd to `newTrailCmd()`:\n```\ncmd.AddCommand(newTrailEnableCmd())\ncmd.AddCommand(newTrailDisableCmd())\n```\n\n`entire trail enable` — sets `strategy_options.trails = true` (or removes the key since true is default). Saves to project settings.\n\n`entire trail disable` — sets `strategy_options.trails = false`. Saves to local settings by default, `--project` flag for project settings.\n\nFollow the pattern from `setup.go` `runEnable`/`runDisable` — load settings, modify `StrategyOptions`, save.\n\n### 3. Guard auto-create with settings check\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go` — `autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart()`\n\nAdd at the top:\n```go\nif settings.IsTrailsDisabled() {\n    return\n}\n```\n\n### 4. Guard push with settings check\n**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/push_common.go` — `PushTrailsBranch()`\n\nAdd check:\n```go\nfunc PushTrailsBranch(remote string) error {\n    if isTrailsDisabled() { return nil }\n    return pushSessionsBranchCommon(remote, paths.TrailsBranchName)\n}\n```\n\nWhere `isTrailsDisabled()` loads settings (same pattern as `isPushSessionsDisabled()`).\n\n### 5. Update CLAUDE.md\nDocument the enable/disable commands and settings key.\n\n## Files Summary\n\n| File | Change |\n|------|--------|\n| `cmd/entire/cli/settings/settings.go` | Add `IsTrailsDisabled()` method + package-level function |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/trail_cmd.go` | Add `newTrailEnableCmd()`, `newTrailDisableCmd()` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go` | Guard `autoCreateTrailOnTurnStart()` with settings check |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/push_common.go` | Guard `PushTrailsBranch()` with settings check |\n| `CLAUDE.md` | Document enable/disable |\n\n## Verification\n\n```bash\nmise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci\n```\n\nManual: `entire trail disable` then verify no trail auto-created on session start.\n"
}
